<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MicroGPT：轻量级 GPT 的新思路</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 17:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47202708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1787</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47202708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compact framework for running GPT-like agents on modest hardware. Emphasizes modular tooling, lightweight memory, and efficient orchestration to enable on-device AI and edge applications. This approach could lower barriers to deploying autonomous AI in real-world products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出在中等算力设备上运行 GPT 风格代理的紧凑框架。强调模块化工具、轻量级内存和高效编排，以实现边缘设备上的 AI 自主执行。该思路可能降低在真实产品中部署自治 AI 的门槛。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47122715' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47122715' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/age-verification' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Age Verification Trap: Verifying age undermines everyone&#x27;s data protection</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>年龄验证陷阱：核验年龄削弱每个人的数据保护</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 06:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47122715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1652</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47122715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47122715'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mandatory age checks often force users to reveal IDs or pass through centralized validators, increasing data exposure. This undermines established data-protection guarantees and creates new attack surfaces for breaches and misuse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>强制年龄核验往往要求用户提供身份信息或通过集中验证服务进行，显著增加数据暴露风险，削弱既有的数据保护保障，并带来新的被滥用与被攻破的风险。文章强调隐私设计的重要性，呼吁采用更具隐私保护的替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=64968905&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C11%2C0%2C239' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=64968905&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C11%2C0%2C239' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47113728' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hawksley.org/2026/02/17/timeframe.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I built Timeframe, our family e-paper dashboard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Timeframe，我们家的电子纸仪表板</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 11:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113728' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1569</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113728' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 362</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47113728'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Timeframe is a family e-paper dashboard that runs on a low-power display for glanceable updates. It aggregates calendar, weather, and household notes, offering a less distracting alternative to smartphone widgets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Timeframe 是一个用于家庭的电子纸仪表板，利用低功耗显示实现一眼可览的更新。它整合日历、天气和家务简报等信息，提供比手机通知更少干扰的主页式信息源。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hawksley.org/img/posts/2026-02-17-timeframe/nook-wide.jpg' data-full='https://hawksley.org/img/posts/2026-02-17-timeframe/nook-wide.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47189650' data-tags='Security,Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47189650' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/sama/status/2027578652477821175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI agrees with Dept. of War to deploy models in their classified network</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 与战争部在其机密网络中部署模型达成协议</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 18:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47189650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1372</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47189650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 636</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47189650'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI reaches an agreement to deploy its models inside the War Department&#x27;s classified network, marking a rare government-use approval for advanced AI. The deal raises questions about security, vendor oversight, and the scale of AI integration into sensitive environments. It signals a potential path for enterprise and defense collaborations, with tight governance requirements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 达成在战争部机密网络中部署其模型的协议，标志着在高敏环境中对先进 AI 的罕见应用获批。该交易引发对安全、供应商监管以及在敏感场景中部署 AI 的规模等问题的讨论。它预示着企业与防务领域日后更紧密的协作之路，同时将伴随严格的治理要求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1904933748015255552/k43GMz63_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1904933748015255552/k43GMz63_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47186677' data-tags='Security,Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47186677' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/secwar/status/2027507717469049070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I am directing the Department of War to designate Anthropic a supply-chain risk</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我指示战争部将 Anthropic 列为供应链风险</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 14:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47186677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1338</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47186677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1062</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47186677'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Department of War reportedly intends to designate Anthropic as a supply-chain risk, which could curb its access to sensitive government projects and influence how agencies screen AI vendors. The move underscores growing government scrutiny of AI suppliers and the need for robust security practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称战争部计划将 Anthropic 列为供应链风险，可能限制其参与敏感政府项目并影响机构对 AI 供应商的评估。这体现政府对 AI 供应商日益严格的审查与对安全实践的强调。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1884589488942321664/5iqq-N_3_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1884589488942321664/5iqq-N_3_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47156925' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://trufflesecurity.com/blog/google-api-keys-werent-secrets-but-then-gemini-changed-the-rules' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google API keys weren&#x27;t secrets, but then Gemini changed the rules</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google API 密钥并非秘密，但 Gemini 改变了规则</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 11:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47156925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1252</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47156925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47156925'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>API keys are often not true secrets, but Gemini&#x27;s approach raises the stakes for credential hygiene. The notes discuss how new model-based tooling and security expectations are reshaping key management practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章讨论 Google API 密钥往往并非真正的秘密，但 Gemini 的新规则提高了对凭证管理的要求，反映出模型驱动工具对安全实践的推动。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47120899' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47120899' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何取消我的 ChatGPT 订阅？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 21:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47190997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1033</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47190997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47190997'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Steps to cancel ChatGPT subscriptions are documented in help; you can navigate to account settings, manage subscription, and confirm cancellation. Your plan will stop auto-renewal and access after the current cycle.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>帮助文档列出取消 ChatGPT 订阅的具体步骤：进入账户设置、管理订阅并确认取消。取消后将停止自动续订，当前周期结束后服务将终止。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47200420' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/OpenAI/status/2027846016423321831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We do not think Anthropic should be designated as a supply chain risk</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>&quot;我们认为 Anthropic 不应被认定为供应链风险&quot;</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 13:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47200420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 797</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47200420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 431</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47200420'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI pushes back on labeling Anthropic as a supply chain risk, arguing the designation misreads vendor risk in AI ecosystems. The exchange underscores ongoing debates about risk classifications for AI vendors and how regulators should approach complex, interconnected AI supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 对将 Anthropic 列为供应链风险的说法提出异议，认为这种定性误读了 AI 生态中的供应商风险。相关讨论凸显了监管机构在对 AI 供应链实施风险分类时的持续辩论与挑战。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47181208' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47181208' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcgamer.com/software/operating-systems/a-new-california-law-says-all-operating-systems-including-linux-need-to-have-some-form-of-age-verification-at-account-setup/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A new California law says all operating systems need to have age verification</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州新法规定所有操作系统在设置账户时需进行年龄验证</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 06:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 794</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 685</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47181208'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California passes a law requiring age verification at OS setup for all devices, including Linux. The move aims to curb underage access but could complicate privacy and OSS distribution, forcing platforms to implement new authentication flows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州通过新法，要求所有操作系统在账户设置时进行年龄验证，覆盖包括 Linux 在内的设备。此举旨在限制未成年人使用，同时可能带来隐私与开源软件分发的合规挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/Z9XbAPKf32a28g9TdTi8dG-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/Z9XbAPKf32a28g9TdTi8dG-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47115805' data-tags='Privacy,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47115805' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/account-restricted-without-warning-google-ai-ultra-oauth-via-openclaw/122778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google restricting Google AI Pro/Ultra subscribers for using OpenClaw</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌限制 Google AI Pro/Ultra 用户使用 OpenClaw</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 15:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47115805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 793</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47115805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 685</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47115805'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google is restricting Google AI Pro/Ultra subscribers from using OpenClaw, a tool some users employ to automate tasks. The move highlights how platform policies can disrupt automation workflows for power users of AI services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，谷歌限制 Google AI Pro/Ultra 用户通过 OpenClaw 等工具进行自动化操作。此举凸显平台政策对高阶用户自动化工作流的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d3qe71uytubmmx.cloudfront.net/original/2X/8/8bfe64fc593eb7baba8df149ebba4457c16ca1f9.png' data-full='https://d3qe71uytubmmx.cloudfront.net/original/2X/8/8bfe64fc593eb7baba8df149ebba4457c16ca1f9.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47110515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 645</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47110515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 267</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47143152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 623</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47143152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 660</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47143152'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple expands domestic manufacturing with a new Mac mini production site in Houston, part of a broader push to reshore chip-and-device production. The move likely creates jobs and reduces supply-chain risk for a popular desktop.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果宣布在休斯顿设立新工厂扩产 Mac mini，这是其推动回流制造的一部分，预计带来就业并降低供应链风险。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47181391' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47181391' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/02/victory-tenth-circuit-finds-fourth-amendment-doesnt-support-broad-search-0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Court finds Fourth Amendment doesn’t support broad search of protesters’ devices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法院裁定第四修正案不支持对抗议者设备的广泛搜索</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 07:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 621</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47181391'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A court held that the Fourth Amendment does not broadly authorize searching protesters’ devices, marking a significant privacy protection for digital evidence. The ruling narrows police power to seize or inspect personal data at demonstrations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法院裁定第四修正案不支持对抗议者设备的广泛搜索，强化了数字隐私保护。这一裁决缩小了警方在示威现场查验个人数据的权限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/protest-2024-2.jpg' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/protest-2024-2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/ai/nano-banana-2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nano Banana 2: Google&#x27;s latest AI image generation model</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47167858'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nano Banana 2 introduces a more efficient image-generation model from Google, aiming to maintain high quality with lower compute. The release underscores Google’s push to scale image synthesis and has implications for developers, content pipelines, and potential on-device capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nano Banana 2 引入一款高效的 AI 图像生成模型，旨在在较低算力下保持质量。此举体现谷歌在图像合成领域的扩张策略，将影响开发者工作流、内容生产链以及设备端能力。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Loops 是一个联邦制、开源的 TikTok 式平台</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 10:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 566</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 384</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47113618'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Loops is a federated, open-source alternative to TikTok, where short videos live on independent servers rather than a single centralized platform. It aims to give users more control and resilience by distributing content across the network.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Loops 是一个联邦化、开源的 TikTok 替代方案，短视频在各自的节点服务器上分发而非集中在一个平台。它旨在通过网络分发提升用户控制权与抗审查性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 08:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197267' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 561</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197267' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47197267'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Obsidian Sync now offers a headless client, enabling automation and scripting without the UI. This unlocks programmatic syncing, custom workflows, and deeper integrations with note pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Obsidian Sync 增添了无头客户端，支持无界面自动化与脚本化操作。用户可以实现编程化同步、定制工作流，并更容易与笔记处理管线整合。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47181211' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/02/27/openai-raises-110b-in-one-of-the-largest-private-funding-rounds-in-history/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI raises $110B on $730B pre-money valuation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 完成 1100 亿美元融资，估值约 7300 亿美元（融资前）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 06:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 557</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 582</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47181211'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI raised a staggering $110B in private funding, valuing the company at about $730B pre-money, underscoring the outsized appetite for AI infrastructure and services. The deal amplifies competitive dynamics and incentives for further AI product bets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 完成约 1100 亿美元的私募融资，融资前估值约 7300 亿美元，凸显对 AI 基础设施与服务的强烈市场需求。这笔交易将推动行业竞争格局与后续产品押注。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47165397' data-tags='AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47165397' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/02/25/tech/anthropic-safety-policy-change' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic ditches its core safety promise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 放弃其核心安全承诺</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 04:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47165397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 551</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47165397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47165397'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports suggest Anthropic is rolling back parts of its safety promises, signaling a shift in how safety is enforced in its products. The move could affect trust, regulatory scrutiny, and risk assessment for AI deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>传言称 Anthropic 正在放弃部分核心安全承诺，显示其在产品安全保障上的策略变化。此举可能影响用户信任、监管审查以及对 AI 部署风险的评估。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/gettyimages-2235057703-20260225133412503.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' data-full='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/gettyimages-2235057703-20260225133412503.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47119210' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ewtnnews.com/vatican/pope-leo-xiv-tells-priests-to-use-their-brains-not-ai-to-write-homilies' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pope tells priests to use their brains, not AI, to write homilies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>教皇让神父用脑子而非AI来写讲道</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 23:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47119210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 551</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47119210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 438</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47119210'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pope Francis urges priests to rely on human judgment rather than AI to craft homilies, signaling cautious boundaries for AI in sacred duties. The stance reflects broader debates about automation&#x27;s role in culturally sensitive work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>教皇方济各呼吁神父在撰写讲道时优先运用人类判断，而非依赖 AI，标志着在宗教敏感工作中对 AI 的审慎边界。其观点映射出自动化在文化性强工作中的广泛辩论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47127396' data-tags='Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47127396' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/02/23/technology/binance-employees-iran-firings.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Binance fired employees who found $1.7B in crypto was sent to Iran</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>币安解雇发现1.7亿美元加密资金流向伊朗的员工</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 11:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47127396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47127396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 256</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47127396'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Whistleblowers reportedly flagged a large crypto transfer to Iran, prompting Binance to terminate the employees who raised the concern. The episode spotlights compliance risk, sanctions evasion, and the potential chilling effect on internal reporting within exchanges. It also raises questions about how exchanges monitor flows and protect employees who surface potential violations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>内部举报者据称发现有价值1.7亿美元的加密资金流向伊朗，随后被币安解雇。事件凸显合规、制裁规避与对内部举报者的保护之间的难题，也引发关于交易所如何监控资金流与保护员工的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static01.nyt.com/images/2026/02/23/multimedia/BINANCE-sub-jgql/BINANCE-sub-jgql-facebookJumbo.jpg' data-full='https://static01.nyt.com/images/2026/02/23/multimedia/BINANCE-sub-jgql/BINANCE-sub-jgql-facebookJumbo.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47152252' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47152252' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/sustainability/boards-policy-regulation/us-orders-diplomats-fight-data-sovereignty-initiatives-2026-02-25/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US orders diplomats to fight data sovereignty initiatives</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国命令外交官对抗数据主权倡议</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 06:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47152252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 534</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47152252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 480</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47152252'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The US is pushing diplomats to push back on data sovereignty laws worldwide, arguing they hinder cross-border data flows and national interests. The move highlights the ongoing policy clash over where data should reside and who can access it.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国推动外交官抵制全球范围内的数据主权法案，声称此类法规阻碍跨境数据流动并影响国家利益。此举凸显了数据应存放在哪、谁有权访问数据等方面的政策博弈。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47205890' data-tags='AI,Business,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47205890' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://99helpers.com/tools/ad-supported-chat' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I built a demo of what AI chat will look like when it&#x27;s “free” and ad-supported</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 聊天若免费且有广告的演示</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 03:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47205890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 532</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47205890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 284</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47205890'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This demo imagines a free, ad supported AI chat and highlights how revenue models could shape latency, limits, and personalization. It flags the tension between free access and data-driven ads, with potential tradeoffs for privacy and user experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这个演示设想了免费且由广告支持的 AI 聊天，揭示了收入模式如何影响响应时延、使用限制和个性化。强调免费获取与数据广告驱动的激励之间的矛盾，可能影响隐私与用户体验。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://moultano.wordpress.com/2026/02/22/the-hunt-for-dark-breakfast/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Hunt for Dark Breakfast</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dark Breakfast 的追踪</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 19:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47176257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 532</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47176257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 180</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47176257'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece explores a concept called Dark Breakfast, examining hidden or overlooked signals within systems and what defenders can learn from them.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章探讨了名为 “Dark Breakfast” 的概念，分析隐藏或被忽视的数据信号在系统中的作用以及对防守方的启示。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://claude.com/contact-sales/claude-for-oss' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Get free Claude max 20x for open-source maintainers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为开源维护者提供 Claude 的免费使用配额，最高可达 20 倍</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 01:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47178371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 532</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47178371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 226</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47178371'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude is offering a free usage quota of up to 20x for open-source maintainers, supporting community-led projects with access to high-quality AI tooling. This helps OSS teams scale their tooling and experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 为开源维护者提供最高可达 20 倍的免费使用配额，支持社区驱动项目使用高质量 AI 工具，助力 OSS 团队扩展工具链。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6889473510b50328dbb70ae6/68c469d23594abeb9ab6ee48_og-claude-generic.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6889473510b50328dbb70ae6/68c469d23594abeb9ab6ee48_og-claude-generic.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47204571' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47204571' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://claude.com/import-memory' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Switch to Claude without starting over</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无需重新开始即可切换到 Claude 的记忆导入</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 23:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 531</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 250</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47204571'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude now offers a memory import feature that lets you switch to Claude without losing prior context, easing migrations and preserving continuity. It reduces migration friction and supports ongoing workflows across models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 新增记忆导入功能，切换至 Claude 时不丢失先前上下文，降低迁移摩擦并保持工作流连续性。为跨模型工作流提供了更顺畅的迁移体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6889473510b50328dbb70ae6/68c469d1859e3e7ecf6c2310_og-claude-finance.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6889473510b50328dbb70ae6/68c469d1859e3e7ecf6c2310_og-claude-finance.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47148454' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47148454' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://code.claude.com/docs/en/remote-control' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code Remote Control</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 远程控制</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 23:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47148454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 523</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47148454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 308</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47148454'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code now supports a Remote Control interface, letting developers drive coding sessions from outside Claude. The feature prompts considerations around access control, session isolation, and security in AI-assisted coding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 现新增远程控制界面，允许开发者在 Claude 外部引导编码会话。此特性带来访问控制、会话隔离和 AI 辅助编码的安全性等议题。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47160226' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/02/tech-companies-shouldnt-be-bullied-doing-surveillance' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tech companies shouldn&#x27;t be bullied into doing surveillance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科技公司不该被迫参与监控</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 16:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47160226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 486</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47160226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47160226'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EFF argues tech firms should resist being coerced into surveillance or data-sharing mandates. It calls for design and policy choices that protect privacy while enabling legitimate data-driven services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>电子前哨基金会主张科技公司不应被强迫参与监控或数据共享。文章强调在保护隐私的前提下，仍要支持合规的数据驱动服务的设计与政策。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47154009' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47154009' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/02/final-2025-data-is-in-us-energy-use-is-up-as-solar-passes-hydro/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Following 35% growth, solar has passed hydro on US grid</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>太阳能在美电网</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 08:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47154009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 484</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47154009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 456</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47154009'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New energy data shows solar generation surpassing hydro on the US grid after years of growth, signaling a major shift in the power mix. The change underscores the importance of storage, grid flexibility, and policy to support renewables.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新能源数据表明，太阳能在多年增长后超越水电，成为美国电网发电的主要来源之一。这一转变凸显储能、柔性电网和可再生能源相关政策的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/GettyImages-2255162141-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/GettyImages-2255162141-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new archive project collects the CIA World Factbook data from 1990 through 2025, making it searchable and exportable. The dataset supports longitudinal geopolitical research and tool-building for data scientists.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gwern.net/doc/iq/high/smpy/1984-clements.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Terence Tao, at 8 years old (1984) [pdf]</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 07:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47123689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 472</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47123689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 303</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47123689'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A PDF captures Terence Tao&#x27;s mathematical work at age eight, offering a rare glimpse into prodigious early talent. The document helps illustrate how early cognitive leaps can foreshadow later extraordinary mathematical achievement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份 PDF 收录了8岁时的 Terence Tao 的数学工作，提供了这位未来菲尔兹奖得主早期天赋的罕见一瞥。该材料揭示了童年阶段的巨大学习跃迁，如何预示后来的卓越成就。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47158975' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47158975' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ben-evans.com/benedictevans/2026/2/19/how-will-openai-compete-nkg2x' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How will OpenAI compete?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 如何竞争？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 14:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47158975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 470</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47158975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 647</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47158975'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ben Evans analyzes how OpenAI could compete: with open ecosystems, strategic partnerships, and differentiating offerings beyond core models. The piece lays out plausible paths for OpenAI to sustain advantage in a crowded AI market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>贝恩·埃文斯分析 OpenAI 的竞争路径，聚焦开放生态、战略伙伴关系以及对核心模型之外的差异化能力。提出在竞争激烈的 AI 市场中维持优势的可行路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/50363cf324ac8e905e7df861/t/6993b59b8517240d2235240a/1771507646532/Frontier%2BLanguage%2BModel%2BIntelligence%2C%2BOver%2BTime%2B%2824%2BJan%2B%2726%29%2B-2.png.webp?format=1500w' data-full='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/50363cf324ac8e905e7df861/t/6993b59b8517240d2235240a/1771507646532/Frontier%2BLanguage%2BModel%2BIntelligence%2C%2BOver%2BTime%2B%2824%2BJan%2B%2726%29%2B-2.png.webp?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47196582' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47196582' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rockoder.com/beyondthecode/cognitive-debt-when-velocity-exceeds-comprehension/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cognitive Debt: When Velocity Exceeds Comprehension</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>認知債務：當速度超過理解力</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 07:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47196582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 469</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47196582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 205</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47196582'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that pushing development velocity too hard creates cognitive debt—hidden mental load and architectural debt that slows future work. It promotes better onboarding, documentation, and mental models to keep velocity sustainable. The takeaway: speed must be matched with clarity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章認為過度追求開發速度會產生認知債務，即藏匿的心理負擔與架構負擔，最終拖慢未來的工作。建議透過更好的上手指引、文件與心智模型，讓速度持續可行。核心觀點是：速度需要與清晰度並行。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.rockoder.com/images/id/id.png' data-full='https://www.rockoder.com/images/id/id.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 10:13 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47184288' data-tags='Career,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47184288' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pseudosingleton.com/leaving-google-improved-my-life/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Leaving Google has actively improved my life</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>离开谷歌后我的生活明显改善</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 11:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47184288' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 467</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47184288' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 258</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47184288'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal essay argues that leaving Google dramatically improved the author&#x27;s life, citing autonomy, privacy, and focus gains. The piece adds to ongoing debates about workplace culture, tech giants, and personal freedom.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在个人随笔中表示离开谷歌后生活明显改善，强调自主性、隐私和专注度的提升。此文为职场文化与科技巨头对个人自由影响的讨论再添讨论点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/herman-1683556668-0.png' data-full='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/herman-1683556668-0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47204964' data-tags='Data,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47204964' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mlu-explain.github.io/decision-tree/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decision trees – the unreasonable power of nested decision rules</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>决策树：嵌套决策规则的强大力量</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 00:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 461</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47204964'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nested decision rules let a decision tree encode nuanced logic with transparent paths, but depth can make them unwieldy. With careful structure and pruning, they remain a practical tool for governable, explainable rules even as data-driven models rise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>嵌套决策规则使决策树能够以透明的路径表达细致的逻辑，但深度增加会使其变得难以维护。通过合适的结构和剪枝，它仍然是可解释性强、可治理的规则工具，即便在数据驱动模型盛行时也有用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mlu-explain.github.io/assets/ogimages/ogimage-decision-tree.png' data-full='https://mlu-explain.github.io/assets/ogimages/ogimage-decision-tree.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47133313' data-tags='AI,Security,Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47133313' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://serverhost.com/blog/firefox-148-launches-with-exciting-ai-kill-switch-feature-and-more-enhancements/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firefox 148 Launches with AI Kill Switch Feature and More Enhancements</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Firefox 148 发布，新增 AI 取消开关等多项增强</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 21:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47133313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 454</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47133313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 383</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47133313'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 148 debuts with an &#x27;AI Kill Switch&#x27; to disable AI features, addressing user control and privacy concerns in browsing helpers. The release also brings several performance and privacy enhancements, reflecting Mozilla&#x27;s stance on user autonomy in an AI-enabled browser.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 148 引入“AI 取消开关”，让用户在浏览器中的 AI 功能可控，回应隐私与信任问题。同时还带来多项性能与隐私改进，体现 Mozilla 在 AI 圈定用户自主权的立场。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 20, 2026 / 14:14 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Diode offers an end-to-end platform for hardware development, letting you design circuits, write firmware, and run simulations in one place. By unifying hardware prototyping steps, it reduces onboarding friction and accelerates experimentation for hobbyists and teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Diode 提供一个端到端的硬件开发平台，集成电路设计、固件编写与仿真。通过统一原型开发流程，降低入门门槛、加速迭代，适合爱好者和团队使用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://keepandroidopen.org/open-letter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open Letter to Google on Mandatory Developer Registration for App Distribution</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>致 Google 的公开信：应用分发需强制开发者注册</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 09:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47139765' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 446</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47139765' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 372</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47139765'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A public letter urges Google to rethink mandatory developer registration for app distribution, arguing it could hamper innovation, transparency, and developer autonomy. The authors advocate more open, privacy-conscious approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公开信呼吁 Google 重新考虑应用分发的强制开发者注册，指其可能削弱创新与开发者自治，主张更开放、隐私友好的分发方式。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47136036' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fortune.com/2026/02/24/discord-peter-thiel-backed-persona-identity-verification-breach/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Discord cuts ties with identity verification software, Persona</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Discord 终止使用 Persona 的身份验证服务</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 03:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47136036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 438</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47136036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 316</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47136036'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discord ends its use of Persona&#x27;s identity verification after concerns over privacy and security, altering how users verify accounts. The move highlights the fragility of third-party identity services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Discord 终止使用 Persona 的身份验证服务，影响用户验证流程；事件凸显了第三方身份服务的脆弱性和隐私安全挑战。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47132388' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47132388' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medicalxpress.com/news/2026-02-blood-boosts-alzheimer-diagnosis-accuracy.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Blood test boosts Alzheimer&#x27;s diagnosis accuracy to 94.5%, clinical study shows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>血液检测将阿尔茨海默病诊断准确率提升至94.5%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 19:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47132388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 434</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47132388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47132388'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A clinical study shows a blood test achieving 94.5% accuracy for Alzheimer&#x27;s diagnosis, potentially enabling earlier and less invasive screening. While promising, questions remain about validation across diverse populations and integration into standard care.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项临床研究显示，一种血液检测能将阿尔茨海默病诊断准确率提升至94.5%，有望实现更早且无创的筛查。尽管前景乐观，仍需在不同人群中的验证以及在临床常规中的落地应用等问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/blood-test-boosts-alzh.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/blood-test-boosts-alzh.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47199781' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47199781' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://venturebeat.com/technology/alibabas-new-open-source-qwen3-5-medium-models-offer-sonnet-4-5-performance' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen3.5 122B and 35B models offer Sonnet 4.5 performance on local computers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen3.5 122B 与 35B 模型在本地电脑上实现 Sonnet 4.5 性能</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 12:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47199781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 433</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47199781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 249</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47199781'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alibaba&#x27;s Qwen3.5 122B and 35B variants achieve Sonnet 4.5-level performance on local hardware, marking a notable efficiency gain for open-source LLMs. This lowers the barrier to deploying large models offline and on edge devices, expanding private, on-device AI options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿里巴巴的 Qwen3.5 122B 与 35B 模型在本地硬件上实现了接近 Sonnet 4.5 的性能，标志着开源大型语言模型的显著效率提升。此举降低了离线和边缘设备上部署大型模型的门槛，扩展了私有化、本地化 AI 的选择。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI-assisted workflow generated a working Wi-Fi driver for a legacy MacBook under FreeBSD, filling a long-standing hardware support gap. The example showcases how AI can accelerate OS hobbyist projects and extend older hardware lifecycles, though it raises questions about driver reliability and licensing considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 帮助下，FreeBSD 为一台老旧 MacBook 自动生成了兼容的 Wi‑Fi 驱动，填补长期的硬件支持空白。此案例展示了 AI 在操作系统爱好者项目中的作用，能延长旧硬件的使用寿命，但也引发关于驱动可靠性与许可的问题。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 08:31 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47153798'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A proposal that reducing bus stops can make urban bus networks faster, cheaper, and more efficient. The approach challenges conventional wisdom about serving everywhere and suggests smarter routing and stop placement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过精简公交停靠点可以让公交网络更快、更省钱且更高效。这一做法挑战以往“全覆盖”的思路，强调更智能的路线与停靠点布局。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>&quot;2&gt;&amp;1&quot; merges standard error and output streams in shells, a fundamental redirection technique. It covers common use cases, caveats, and how it behaves in pipelines.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.popsci.com/technology/robot-vacuum-army/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Man accidentally gains control of 7k robot vacuums</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>男子意外控制了 7,000 台机器人吸尘器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 06:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47111400' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 413</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47111400' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47111400'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security misconfiguration allowed one person to control around 7,000 robot vacuums, exposing IoT risk at scale. The incident underscores why strong authentication, firmware hardening, and vendor safeguards matter for consumer devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次安全配置错误让一人能够控制约7000台机器人吸尘器，暴露了大规模物联网风险。事件强调了强认证、固件加固和厂商防护对消费级设备的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.popsci.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/robot_army.jpg?quality=85&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://www.popsci.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/robot_army.jpg?quality=85&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47167763' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47167763' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ndss-symposium.org/wp-content/uploads/2026-f1282-paper.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AirSnitch: Demystifying and breaking client isolation in Wi-Fi networks [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AirSnitch：揭示并突破 Wi-Fi 网络中的客户端隔离</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 07:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47167763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 407</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47167763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47167763'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AirSnitch demonstrates practical methods to defeat client isolation in Wi‑Fi networks, revealing attack vectors. The work motivates stronger AP isolation and mitigations, underscoring ongoing security concerns in wireless deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AirSnitch 的研究揭示了 Wi‑Fi 客户端隔离的可被利用的缺口，指出了改进接入点隔离和其他缓解措施的必要性。对无线网络安全的警示意义明显。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47075455' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/solid-state-lidar-microvision-adas' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sub-$200 Lidar could reshuffle auto sensor economics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不足200美元的激光雷达或将重塑汽车传感器经济</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 19, 2026 / 08:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47075455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 401</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47075455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 537</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47075455'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cheaper sub-$200 solid-state lidar could reshape the economics of automotive sensing, lowering per-vehicle sensor costs and accelerating ADAS adoption. But manufacturers still face hurdles in performance, reliability, and scalable production that will determine how quickly the market shifts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>成本低廉的固态激光雷达可能重塑汽车传感器的经济格局，显著降低单车传感器成本并推动高级驾驶辅助系统的普及。然而要实现大规模生产，性能、可靠性与产能扩张仍是关键挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://read.technically.dev/p/vibe-coding-and-the-maker-movement' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Will vibe coding end like the maker movement?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vibe 编码会像创客运动一样终结吗？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 08:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47167931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 399</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47167931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 424</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47167931'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vibe coding is gaining attention, but questions remain about its long-term value beyond hype and maker culture nostalgia. The piece weighs whether a culture-driven approach to programming can scale or endure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vibe 编码的热潮引发对其长期价值的质疑：是否只是一时的炒作，还是能否支撑可持续的开发文化与工具生态？</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47200879' data-tags='Politics,Security,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47200879' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npr.org/2026/02/28/1123499337/iran-israel-ayatollah-ali-khamenei-killed' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iran&#x27;s Ayatollah Ali Khamenei is killed in Israeli strike, ending 36-year rule</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗最高领袖哈梅内伊在以色列袭击中被击毙，结束36年统治</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 14:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47200879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 397</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47200879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 747</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47200879'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports confirm the assassination of Iran&#x27;s top leader, triggering a potential power vacuum and regional instability. The aftermath could reshape Middle East geopolitics and security policy, with implications for sanctions, energy markets, and cyber strategy that tech professionals monitor for risk assessment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，伊朗最高领袖哈梅内伊在以色列发动的袭击中被击毙，可能引发权力真空与区域不稳定。后果可能重塑中东地缘政治、安保政策，并对制裁、能源市场与网络安全策略产生影响，科技从业者需据此评估风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.npr.org/assets/img/2022/09/16/ap17079498857800_wide-9307243360e1a8534d95eb01476568e7f46a2b4a.jpg?s=1400&amp;c=85&amp;f=jpeg' data-full='https://media.npr.org/assets/img/2022/09/16/ap17079498857800_wide-9307243360e1a8534d95eb01476568e7f46a2b4a.jpg?s=1400&amp;c=85&amp;f=jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://magicalmushroom.com/index' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Magical Mushroom – Europe&#x27;s first industrial-scale mycelium packaging producer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Magical Mushroom：欧洲首家工业规模菌丝体包装生产商</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 23:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47119274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 385</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47119274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 123</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47119274'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe&#x27;s first industrial-scale mycelium packaging producer aims to replace plastics with fungal-based materials. The move signals growing interest in biodegradable, low-carbon packaging, though commercialization, performance, and supply-chain hurdles remain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲首家以菌丝体为材料的工业级包装生产商正推动用真菌材料替代塑料包装，强调可降解、低碳环保的潜力。尽管市场前景乐观，规模化生产、性能表现和供应链仍面临挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://a.storyblok.com/f/142449/2500x1667/4cab6ab4ac/lm0_9542_rt.jpg' data-full='https://a.storyblok.com/f/142449/2500x1667/4cab6ab4ac/lm0_9542_rt.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
