<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47161160' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/02/ram-now-represents-35-percent-of-bill-of-materials-for-hp-pcs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RAM now represents 35 percent of bill of materials for HP PCs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RAM 已占 HP PC 物料成本的 35%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 18:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47161160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 381</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47161160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 335</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47161160'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RAM now accounts for 35% of HP PC BOM, reflecting rising memory costs and demand; this squeezes margins and may push design choices toward more cost-effective memory configurations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RAM 现已占据 HP PC 物料成本的约 35%，反映出内存成本上升与需求强劲。这可能压缩利润率并驱动向更具性价比的内存配置调整。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/GettyImages-2252687789-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/GettyImages-2252687789-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47125349' data-tags='Hardware,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47125349' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/china/asml-unveils-euv-light-source-advance-that-could-yield-50-more-chips-by-2030-2026-02-23/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ASML unveils EUV light source advance that could yield 50% more chips by 2030</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ASML 公布 EUV 光源的进展，到2030年或使芯片产量提升50%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 09:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47125349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 368</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47125349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47125349'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ASML reveals a next-generation EUV light source that could boost chip yields by up to 50% by 2030, easing the roadmap pressure for leading-node fabrication. If realized, this could lower costs and extend Moore&#x27;s law-like trajectories, though integration into fabs will take years.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ASML 公布下一代 EUV 光源技术，若商用可实现到2030年产出提升约50%，将缓解先进制程的产能压力并推动成本下降。实现仍需时间，在实际 fabs 的整合也会面临挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47136611' data-tags='Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47136611' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hacks.mozilla.org/2026/02/goodbye-innerhtml-hello-sethtml-stronger-xss-protection-in-firefox-148/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Goodbye InnerHTML, Hello SetHTML: Stronger XSS Protection in Firefox 148</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>告别 InnerHTML，迎来 SetHTML：Firefox 148 提升 XSS 防护</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 05:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47136611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 366</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47136611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47136611'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 148 introduces SetHTML for safer handling of HTML to reduce XSS risks, moving away from innerHTML in many contexts. Developers are advised on updating code to use safer APIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 148 引入 SetHTML，提升对 HTML 的安全处理，降低 XSS 风险，逐步替代众多场景中的 innerHTML；开发者需要改用更安全的 API。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hacks.mozilla.org/wp-content/themes/Hax/img/hacks-meta-image.jpg' data-full='https://hacks.mozilla.org/wp-content/themes/Hax/img/hacks-meta-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47125374' data-tags='AI,Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47125374' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/guides/agentic-engineering-patterns/code-is-cheap/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Writing code is cheap now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>写代码现在很便宜</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 09:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47125374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 362</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47125374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 467</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47125374'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cheap code is accelerating prototyping and experimentation as AI copilots handle boilerplate and refactoring. The piece argues this lowers barriers for building, but warns that maintainability and security still require strong design — new patterns like agentic engineering emerge to manage complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着 AI 助手接手大量模板化与重复性编码，写代码成本显著降低，开发速度加快。文章提醒人们，尽管成本下降，代码质量、维护性和安全性仍需依赖良好设计，并提出将代理式工程等新模式用于管理复杂性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47128138' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47128138' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opper.ai/blog/car-wash-test' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“Car Wash” test with 53 models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“Car Wash”测试覆盖53种模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 12:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47128138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 361</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47128138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 433</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47128138'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The &#x27;Car Wash&#x27; test benchmark evaluates 53 machine learning models, highlighting differences in robustness under common refactoring or input perturbations. The results illustrate how model quality varies across architectures and training regimes, stressing the need for robust evaluation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>“Car Wash”测试对53个模型进行了评估，凸显在常见扰动下的鲁棒性差异。结果揭示了不同架构和训练策略的模型在质量上的差异，强调需要更稳健的评估标准。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opper.ai/images/opper-og.jpg' data-full='https://opper.ai/images/opper-og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47208398' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47208398' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ejholmes.github.io/2026/02/28/mcp-is-dead-long-live-the-cli.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When does MCP make sense vs CLI?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MCP 何时有意义，CLI 何时更合适？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 08:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47208398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 356</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47208398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47208398'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that MCP is largely obsolete, while the CLI remains the flexible and reliable tool for automation. It contrasts scenarios where a higher-level protocol might be tempting against the practicality and ecosystem support of the CLI. Takeaway: design tooling with a CLI-first mindset and reserve MCP for niche orchestration tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 MCP 基本过时，CLI 仍然是自动化的灵活且可信赖的工具。对比了在需要重复编排时 MCP 可能有用的情景与 CLI 的可用性和生态系统优势。核心观点是优先以 CLI 为中心设计工具，MCP 仅在少数专业编排任务中才有价值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/our-agreement-with-the-department-of-war' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Our Agreement with the Department of War</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们与战争部的协议</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 12:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47199948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 349</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47199948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 254</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47199948'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI shares an agreement with the Department of War, framing how the organization considers defense-related deployment and governance of its AI technology. The document sheds light on safety commitments and the boundaries of collaboration, highlighting tensions between innovation and national security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 公布了与战争部的协议，阐明公司在军事用途及相关治理方面的立场与安排。该文件揭示了在安全承诺和协作边界方面的考量，以及创新与国家安全之间的张力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blogs.windows.com/windows-insider/2026/01/21/notepad-and-paint-updates-begin-rolling-out-to-windows-insiders/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Windows 11 Notepad to support Markdown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 11 记事本将支持 Markdown</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 09:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47154399' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 349</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47154399' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 523</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47154399'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Windows Notepad gains native Markdown support, signaling Microsoft&#x27;s push toward developer-friendly tiny tools. The feature makes quick notes with formatting easier for programmers and writers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Windows 记事本将原生支持 Markdown，体现微软在小型开发工具上的友好取向。此功能让程序员和作者在记笔记时能更方便地处理格式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogs.windows.com/wp-content/themes/microsoft-stories-theme/img/theme/logos/windows.jpg' data-full='https://blogs.windows.com/wp-content/themes/microsoft-stories-theme/img/theme/logos/windows.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47206824' data-tags='AI,Career,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47206824' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ivanturkovic.com/2026/02/25/ai-made-writing-code-easier-engineering-harder/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Made Writing Code Easier. It Made Being an Engineer Harder</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 让写代码更容易，却让工程师更难</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 06:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 343</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47206824'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI can accelerate code generation and boilerplate, but it shifts the burden to engineers for design, vetting, and integration. The result is higher cognitive load and accountability as teams balance speed with reliability, security, and maintainability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 在生成代码和处理重复任务方面提升了效率，但也把设计、审核与整合的责任压在工程师身上。工作强度和对输出质量的要求提高，需要在速度、可靠性与安全性之间取得平衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47155526' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47155526' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ad-si/Woxi' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Woxi: Wolfram Mathematica Reimplementation in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Woxi：用 Rust 重实现 Wolfram Mathematica</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 10:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47155526' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 342</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47155526' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47155526'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Woxi is a Rust-based reimplementation of Wolfram Mathematica, aiming to deliver high-performance symbolic computation. It exemplifies efforts to democratize advanced math tooling while highlighting the challenges of reimplementing a mature system.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Woxi 用 Rust 重实现 Wolfram Mathematica，目标是提供高性能的符号计算工具。该项目展示了让高端数学工具更易得的努力，同时也揭示了重实现一个成熟系统的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7bb99285ffac42a49498a0c758e3091f8fce5f372a6628db595f7821bd10b06c/ad-si/Woxi' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7bb99285ffac42a49498a0c758e3091f8fce5f372a6628db595f7821bd10b06c/ad-si/Woxi' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mercury 2 通过扩散技术为大型语言模型提供快速推理，旨在提升链式推理速度并降低算力需求。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 18, 2026 / 13:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47067031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 337</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47067031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47067031'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective piece that uses a hypothetical journey to a microwave as a stand-in for exploring alternate timelines and the consequences of small tech decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇个人随笔，以微波炉作为隐喻，探讨如何通过日常选择进入不同的时间线。作者反思理性决策与科技进展对人生轨迹的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47139716' data-tags='Privacy,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47139716' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonlermen.substack.com/p/large-scale-online-deanonymization' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Large-Scale Online Deanonymization with LLMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大规模在线去匿名化与大语言模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 09:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47139716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 330</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47139716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 233</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47139716'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that large language models, combined with publicly available data, enable dramatic scale in online deanonymization, raising privacy and security concerns. It discusses potential attack surfaces and mitigation strategies, highlighting the tension between AI capabilities and individual anonymity on the open web.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，结合大型语言模型和公开数据，能够在大规模层面开展在线去匿名化，引发隐私与安全担忧。分析了可能的攻击面与缓解策略，强调了AI能力与个人隐私保护之间的矛盾。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47200904' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47200904' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dl.acm.org/doi/fullHtml/10.1145/238386.238611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Windows 95 user interface: A case study in usability engineering (1996)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 95 用户界面：可用性工程案例研究（1996）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 14:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47200904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 329</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47200904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47200904'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An early look at how Windows 95&#x27;s UI benefited from usability engineering, including user testing, task-focused layouts, and predictable navigation. The case demonstrates the value of iterative feedback for complex desktop interfaces, underscoring ongoing trade-offs between feature richness and learnability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>早期对 Windows 95 用户界面的分析，揭示其在可用性工程方面的实践，如用户测试、以任务为中心的布局和可预测的导航。该案例展示了迭代反馈在处理复杂桌面界面中的价值，并强调功能丰富与易学易用之间的权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npr.org/2026/02/27/nx-s1-5729118/trump-anthropic-pentagon-openai-ai-weapons-ban' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>President Trump bans Anthropic from use in government systems</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 13:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47186031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 323</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47186031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 212</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47186031'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>President Trump bans Anthropic from use in government systems, signaling a policy move that could affect procurement and access to AI tools for public sector. It illustrates how political shifts shape AI vendor relationships.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特朗普总统宣布禁止 Anthropic 在政府系统中使用，可能影响公共部门的采购与对 AI 工具的访问。这一定性转变也凸显政治因素对 AI 供应商关系的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nanoclaw.dev/blog/nanoclaw-security-model' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t trust AI agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>别信任 AI 代理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 04:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47194611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 321</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47194611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47194611'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of the reliability and safety risks of autonomous AI agents, showing why you shouldn&#x27;t over-rely on them for critical tasks. The piece advocates robust architectures, verification, and tamper-resistance to prevent misbehavior or exploitation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨自治 AI 代理的可靠性与安全风险，解释为何不应过度依赖它们完成关键任务。文章主张构建更强健的架构、可验证性与防篡改设计，以防止误操作或被利用。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47131225' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.guidelabs.ai/post/steerling-8b-base-model-release/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Steerling-8B, a language model that can explain any token it generates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Steerling-8B，一种能解释其生成的每个标记的语言模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 16:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47131225' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47131225' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47131225'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Steerling-8B offers a built-in explainability layer that can justify each token it generates, addressing a key pain point in LLM trust and debugging. The project explores how model transparency can inform better prompting, debugging, and safety checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Steerling-8B 提供内置的可解释性层，能够为其生成的每个标记给出解释，有助于提升 LLM 的可信度、调试与安全性。该项目探索透明度在提示设计和错误排查中的应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.guidelabs.ai/logo/guidelabs-social-media-thumbnail.png' data-full='https://www.guidelabs.ai/logo/guidelabs-social-media-thumbnail.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47123631' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/pgdogdev/pgdog' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: PgDog – Scale Postgres without changing the app</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PgDog — 无需改动应用就能扩展 PostgreSQL</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 07:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47123631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47123631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47123631'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PgDog offers a method to scale PostgreSQL capacity without requiring changes to client apps, potentially via a sharding/proxy approach. If effective, it could simplify scaling for existing deployments and reduce migration risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PgDog 提供一种在不修改客户端应用的前提下扩大 PostgreSQL 容量的方法，可能通过代理层或分片实现。若有效，将简化现有部署的扩容工作，降低迁移风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/50266dc70774ce46450e2515142c67484fdd9711f031eace4d4193fbc522009d/pgdogdev/pgdog' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/50266dc70774ce46450e2515142c67484fdd9711f031eace4d4193fbc522009d/pgdogdev/pgdog' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47114678' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47114678' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://idiallo.com/blog/installed-single-turnstile-for-security-theater' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We installed a single turnstile to feel secure</a></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ivanturkovic.com/2026/01/22/history-software-simplification-cobol-ai-hype/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Eternal Promise: A History of Attempts to Eliminate Programmers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>永恒的承诺：试图消灭程序员的历史</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 21:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47147597' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 308</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47147597' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 211</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47147597'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Software has long promised to automate away developers, and this history shows why that promise repeatedly fails. The piece surveys milestones from early auto-generation to modern AI-assisted tooling, arguing that complexity and creative problem-solving keep programmers indispensable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>软件领域长期被承诺要自动化淘汰程序员，但这一承诺屡屡难以实现。本文梳理了从早期自动生成代码到现代 AI 辅助工具的里程碑，指出复杂性和人类解决问题的能力使程序员不可替代。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47146487' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47146487' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.janestreet.com/can-you-reverse-engineer-our-neural-network/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can you reverse engineer our neural network?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你能反向工程我们的神经网络吗？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 18:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47146487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47146487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 193</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47146487'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece examines how much you can infer about a trained neural network from its outputs, including architecture, weights, and training data. It highlights practical limits and security implications for protecting IP and guarding against model extraction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨仅凭输出能否推断出一个训练完成的神经网络的架构、权重和训练数据，以及这对知识产权保护和模型提取安全的现实限制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.janestreet.com/can-you-reverse-engineer-our-neural-network/puzzle.png' data-full='https://blog.janestreet.com/can-you-reverse-engineer-our-neural-network/puzzle.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47129727' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://writings.stephenwolfram.com/2026/02/making-wolfram-tech-available-as-a-foundation-tool-for-llm-systems/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making Wolfram tech available as a foundation tool for LLM systems</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让 Wolfram 技术成为 LLM 系统的基础工具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 14:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47129727' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 301</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47129727' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47129727'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wolfram&#x27;s tech is being embedded as a foundational tool for LLM systems, enabling precise computation, data lookup, and symbolic reasoning within AI assistants. This approach enhances accuracy and explainability while shaping how future copilots reason about mathematics, science, and data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wolfram 的计算与知识图谱能力正被整合进 LLM 系统，作为基础工具以提供精确计算、数据检索与符号推理。此举提升了答案的准确性与可解释性，并将塑造未来助手在数学、科学与数据领域的推理方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://content.wolfram.com/sites/43/2026/02/FoundationTool-seo-v2a.png' data-full='https://content.wolfram.com/sites/43/2026/02/FoundationTool-seo-v2a.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Iranian students staged what appears to be the largest anti-government protests since a deadly crackdown, signaling continued unrest and potential for online mobilization. The demonstrations spotlight how students remain a catalyst for political change, despite restrictions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伊朗学生举行自致命镇压以来规模最大的反政府抗议，显示持续的社会动荡与线上动员的潜力。示威凸显学生群体仍是推动政治变革的重要推动力，尽管面临限制。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 12:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47128645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 291</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47128645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Baking a pie every day for a year became a catalyst for routine, creativity, and personal growth, revealing how small, repeatable practices can unlock new habits. The experiment offers transferable lessons on discipline, iteration, and finding joy in craft.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>每天烤一个馅饼持续一年成为培养日常规律、创造力与个人成长的催化剂，展示了以小而重复的练习可以养成新习惯。此实验提供可迁移的自律、迭代与在制作中发现乐趣的启示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dork.dev/posts/2026-02-20-ported-coreboot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Ported Coreboot to the ThinkPad X270</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我已将 Coreboot 移植到 ThinkPad X270</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 15:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47130860' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 289</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47130860' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47130860'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal project ports Coreboot to the ThinkPad X270, replacing the official BIOS with open-source firmware. It demonstrates the feasibility and challenges of reviving older hardware with modern, auditable firmware and can improve boot speed and security, though feature support may lag.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>个人项目将 Coreboot 移植到 ThinkPad X270，用开源固件取代原厂 BIOS。此举展示了在较旧硬件上实现可验证、快速启动与更高安全性的可行性，同时也存在驱动、硬件兼容性等挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科技贵族试图建造二十一世纪的法西斯国家</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 10:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47198912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 286</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47198912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47198912'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that a tech-elite class seeks to consolidate power through surveillance and platform control, linking economic disparity with political risk. It calls for vigilance about governance, antitrust, and civic safeguards in the age of digital power.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张科技精英阶层正通过监控与平台控制来巩固权力，将经济不平等与政治风险联系在一起。呼吁在数字时代加强治理、反垄断与公民防护。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47130208' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47130208' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gizmodo.com/ai-added-basically-zero-to-us-economic-growth-last-year-goldman-sachs-says-2000725380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Added &#x27;Basically Zero&#x27; to US Economic Growth Last Year, Goldman Sachs Says</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>高盛称 AI 对去年美国经济增长几乎没有贡献</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 14:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47130208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 283</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47130208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47130208'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Goldman Sachs argues that AI contributed essentially zero to U.S. economic growth last year, despite hype around AI-driven productivity. The data suggests that the macro wind is still dominated by fundamentals like labor, capital, and investment cycles, with AI&#x27;s productivity gains taking time to materialize.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>高盛表示，尽管 AI 热潮高涨，但去年美国经济增长几乎没有来自 AI 的贡献，AI 的生产率提升仍在滞后期。结论强调经济增长仍受劳动、资本和投资周期等基本因素驱动，AI 的收益需要时间显现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gizmodo.com/app/uploads/2024/08/samaltman.png' data-full='https://gizmodo.com/app/uploads/2024/08/samaltman.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.beyondthejoke.co.uk/content/17193/red-dwarf-rob-grant' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rob Grant, creator of Red Dwarf, has died</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 11:26 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47207404' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47207404' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedaily.com/releases/2026/02/260228093456.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New iron nanomaterial wipes out cancer cells without harming healthy tissue</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新型铁基纳米材料可消灭癌细胞而不伤及健康组织</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 07:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47207404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 278</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47207404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47207404'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In early-stage studies, a new iron-based nanomaterial appears to selectively kill cancer cells while sparing healthy tissue. The material leverages targeted uptake and triggering mechanisms to minimize collateral damage, representing a potential path toward safer, targeted cancer therapies. If validated clinically, it could complement or replace more toxic treatments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在早期研究阶段，一种新型铁基纳米材料似乎能够选择性地杀死癌细胞，同时尽量保护健康组织。该材料通过定向摄取和触发机制来降低副作用，为更安全的靶向癌症治疗提供潜在途径。一旦临床验证，可能成为现有更毒治疗的有力补充或替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sciencedaily.com/images/1920/cancer-cell-self-destructing.webp' data-full='https://www.sciencedaily.com/images/1920/cancer-cell-self-destructing.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A seminal sociological essay on how corrupt practices become normalized in organizations, blurring ethics and triggering governance failures. The work remains influential for understanding corporate culture and change.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇关于组织内部腐败如何被常态化、模糊伦理并引发治理失败的里程碑式社会学论文。该研究对理解企业文化与组织变革具有持续影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 14:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47115574' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 263</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47115574' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece shares the folding steps for the Blade Runner origami unicorn from 1996, a fan-favorite model linked to the film&#x27;s lore. It also reflects on how an iconic prop can inspire an accessible paper-folding project that blends pop culture with craft.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中提供了 1996 年《银翼杀手》中的折纸独角兽的折法，同时讨论这一标志性模型在粉丝文化中的影响，以及将经典元素转化为可实践的折纸项目的魅力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lyra.horse/x86css/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: X86CSS – An x86 CPU emulator written in CSS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>X86CSS 是一个用 CSS 实现的 x86 CPU 模拟器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 18:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47132102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 261</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47132102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47132102'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>X86CSS implements an x86 CPU emulator purely in CSS, a creative exercise that pushes the boundaries of CSS as a computational medium. While not practical for real workloads, it offers an entertaining demonstration of declarative rendering, and educational value for understanding CPU instruction flow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>X86CSS 是一个完全用 CSS 实现的 x86 CPU 模拟器，展现了将 CSS 推向计算用途的创造性尝试。尽管在实际性能上不可比拟，但它对理解指令执行流程具有教育意义且具趣味性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 05:07 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在军事仿真游戏中，AI 仍倾向建议核打击，暴露了模型偏见与政策缺口。文章呼吁加强安全护栏和情景测试。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47189749' data-tags='Security,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47189749' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.timcappalli.me/p/passkeys-prf-warning/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t use passkeys for encrypting user data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不要用 Passkeys 对用户数据进行加密</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 19:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47189749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 259</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47189749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47189749'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Passkeys are designed for authentication, not as encryption keys. Using them to encrypt data complicates key management, revocation, and sharing, creating security and compliance risks. The piece advocates using proper encryption keys or a KMS instead of reusing authentication material for data confidentiality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Passkeys 旨在用于身份验证，而非加密密钥。将其用于数据加密会使密钥管理、撤销与共享变得复杂，带来安全与合规风险。应使用专门的加密密钥或密钥管理服务来保护数据机密性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.timcappalli.me/p/passkeys-prf-warning/featured.jpg' data-full='https://blog.timcappalli.me/p/passkeys-prf-warning/featured.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47128960' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thenevadaindependent.com/article/vegas-police-are-big-users-of-license-plate-readers-public-has-little-input-because-its-a-gift' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flock cameras gifted by Horowitz Foundation, avoiding public oversight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Horowitz 基金赠送的 Flock 摄像头，规避公众监督</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 13:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47128960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47128960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47128960'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Public oversight is circumvented as a philanthropic gift funds Flock surveillance cameras, raising concerns about governance, transparency, and civil liberties. The arrangement highlights how philanthropy can extend private surveillance influence without democratic checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一笔来自捐赠基金的资助让 Flock 摄像头投入使用，绕过了公众监督。此举引发对治理、透明度与公民自由的担忧，凸显慈善捐赠在没有民主制衡的情况下可能扩张私营监控。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47181380' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.understandingai.org/p/the-pentagon-is-making-a-mistake' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Pentagon is making a mistake by threatening Anthropic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>五角大楼威胁 Anthropic 是一个错误</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 07:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 255</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 227</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47181380'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Understanding AI argues the Pentagon is making a mistake by threatening Anthropic, suggesting such pressure could backfire by limiting access to trusted AI tooling for defense programs. The piece advocates more collaborative policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 AI 的理解组织认为五角大楼威胁 Anthropic 是一个错误，认为此举可能适得其反，限制防务项目对可信 AI 工具的获取。文章主张应采用更协作的政策。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47211249' data-tags='AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47211249' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.chrome.com/blog/webmcp-epp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WebMCP is available for early preview</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WebMCP 已进入早期预览</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 14:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47211249' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 254</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47211249' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47211249'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome is previewing WebMCP, a new protocol intended to standardize how in-browser machine learning workloads are executed and coordinated. The early preview suggests decoupling model runtime from apps, which could enable portable, interoperable AI workloads across web apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome 正在公开 WebMCP 的早期预览，该新协议旨在标准化浏览器中机器学习工作负载的执行与协调。预览显示将模型运行时与应用逻辑解耦，可能为网页端 AI 任务带来更强的跨环境互操作性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://developer.chrome.com/static/blog/webmcp-epp/image/cover.png' data-full='https://developer.chrome.com/static/blog/webmcp-epp/image/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://growingswe.com/blog/microgpt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microgpt explained interactively</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微型 GPT 的交互式讲解</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 01:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47205208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 252</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47205208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MicroGPT shows how compact language-model agents can orchestrate tools and tasks with lightweight prompts. The interactive guide walks through architecture, tradeoffs, and practical patterns for building small, efficient AI assistants. It helps developers reason about scalability and resource use when moving beyond monolithic models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MicroGPT 展示了紧凑型语言模型代理如何通过简洁提示来编排工具和任务。互动指南带你了解架构、取舍和构建小型高效 AI 助手的实用模式，帮助开发者在从单一大模型向分布式、低资源方案演进时评估可扩展性与资源使用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.codemine.be/posts/2026/20260222-be-quiet/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLM=True</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LLM=True</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 01:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47149151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 247</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47149151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47149151'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Be skeptical of headlines claiming LLMs &#x27;know&#x27; the truth; the post argues that current LLMs lack reliable grounding and that truthfulness must be engineered with external tools or verification. It offers a framework for evaluating truthfulness beyond shiny benchmarks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对声称大模型“知道真相”的说法保持怀疑；文章认为当前的模型缺乏可靠的证据基础，真实可信需要借助外部工具和验证。并给出了超越炫耀性基准的真值评估框架。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.codemine.be/images/2026/be-quiet/agent_dogs.jpg' data-full='https://blog.codemine.be/images/2026/be-quiet/agent_dogs.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 08:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47112299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 245</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47112299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A glass painter hammers portraits into glass, creating fractured, luminous textures that blend sculpture and painting. The technique yields a distinctive, tactile artwork with unique light interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这位艺术家通过敲击玻璃来绘制肖像，产生独特的碎裂质感。该技法结合雕塑与绘画，呈现出带有触觉与光影互动的艺术美。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47170030' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47170030' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anadim/AdderBoard' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Smallest transformer that can add two 10-digit numbers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>能够把两组十位数相加的最小 Transformer</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 10:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47170030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 230</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47170030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47170030'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A minimalist Transformer architecture demonstrates it can reliably add two 10-digit integers, illustrating the surprising arithmetic capability of language models. The project, AdderBoard, benchmarks how small a model can be to perform precise arithmetic and hints at why transformer-based reasoning can be surprisingly efficient on narrow tasks. It also invites discussion on hardware constraints and reproducibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个极简的 Transformer 架构能够可靠地把两组十位整数相加，展示了语言模型在算术任务上的意外能力。该项目 AdderBoard 评估了多小的模型就能完成精确计算，并就 transformer 推理在特定任务中的效率展开讨论。也引发对硬件约束与可复现性的思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/20d4345a4d577dcacbcd4226774040792b3ea6ab274a24caf8be05bde7071211/anadim/AdderBoard' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/20d4345a4d577dcacbcd4226774040792b3ea6ab274a24caf8be05bde7071211/anadim/AdderBoard' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Unsloth Dynamic 2.0 GGUFs</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 00:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47192505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 228</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47192505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47192505'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Unsloth Dynamic 2.0 introduces an updated GGUF format that supports dynamic loading and on-demand quantization for large language model weights. The update improves loading speed and memory efficiency on consumer hardware, enabling larger models to run with fewer GPUs. This matters for developers and researchers building on-device AI tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Unsloth Dynamic 2.0 引入更新的 GGUF 格式，支持权重的动态加载和按需量化。此改动提升了在消费级硬件上的加载速度与内存利用效率，使更大模型在更少显卡上也能运行。对在设备端构建 AI 工具链的开发者和研究者尤为相关。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://unsloth.ai/docs/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F2815821428-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Forganizations%252FHpyELzcNe0topgVLGCZY%252Fsites%252Fsite_mXXTe%252Fsocialpreview%252FOeFDVbzp4cgwaId7PbvL%252Funsloth%2520docs%2520pic.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df34a6bcc-db15-449a-bc2d-e0e8f52fe953&amp;width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;sign=b1ca68fa&amp;sv=2' data-full='https://unsloth.ai/docs/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F2815821428-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Forganizations%252FHpyELzcNe0topgVLGCZY%252Fsites%252Fsite_mXXTe%252Fsocialpreview%252FOeFDVbzp4cgwaId7PbvL%252Funsloth%2520docs%2520pic.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df34a6bcc-db15-449a-bc2d-e0e8f52fe953&amp;width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;sign=b1ca68fa&amp;sv=2' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mendral.com/blog/llms-are-good-at-sql' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We gave terabytes of CI logs to an LLM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们把 TB 级持续集成日志输入到一个大型语言模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 07:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47181801'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>We fed terabytes of CI logs to a large language model to explore its usefulness for debugging and log querying, showing LLMs can parse and reason over software pipelines. Still, it raises concerns about data leakage and model privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>我们将 TB 级持续集成日志输入到一个大型语言模型，以探索其在调试和日志查询中的应用潜力，显示 LLM 可以对软件流水线进行解析和推理，但也引发数据外泄与隐私的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mendral.com/og?title=LLMs%20Are%20Good%20at%20SQL.%20We%20Gave%20Ours%20Terabytes%20of%20CI%20Logs.' data-full='https://mendral.com/og?title=LLMs%20Are%20Good%20at%20SQL.%20We%20Gave%20Ours%20Terabytes%20of%20CI%20Logs.' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/c3d/db48x/commit/7819972b641ac808d46c54d3f5d1df70d706d286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open source calculator firmware DB48X forbids CA/CO use due to age verification</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源计算器固件 DB48X 因年龄验证而禁止在 CA/CO 使用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 07:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181753' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181753' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47181753'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open source calculator firmware DB48X forbids CA/CO use due to age verification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开源计算器固件 DB48X 因年龄验证要求，禁止在加州和科罗拉多州使用，体现了地区法令对硬件固件分发的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://forums.atariage.com/topic/380883-unix99-a-unix-like-os-for-the-ti-994a/page/5/#findComment-5713334' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UNIX99, a UNIX-like OS for the TI-99/4A (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>UNIX99，为 TI-99/4A 提供类 UNIX 操作系统</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 12:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47127986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47127986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47127986'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UNIX99 is a UNIX-like OS for the TI-99/4A, reviving classic hardware with modern OS concepts. The project showcases the enduring appeal of retro machines and the feasibility of reimagining vintage tech with contemporary tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>UNIX99 为 TI-99/4A 提供了类 UNIX 操作系统，借助现代工具对经典硬件进行再活化。该项目体现了对复古计算机的持续热情，以及用当代技术改造老硬件的可行性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47197595' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47197595' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gist.github.com/dollspace-gay/d8d3bc3ecf4188df049d7a4726bb2a00' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Verified Spec-Driven Development (VSDD)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>经验证的规范驱动开发（VSDD）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 08:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197595' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 203</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197595' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47197595'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Verified Spec-Driven Development advocates anchoring software work to verified specifications, then deriving tests and implementations from them. It emphasizes keeping specs honest, aligning teams, and reducing misinterpretation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>经验证的规范驱动开发主张以经过验证的规范为基石，进而推导测试与实现，强调保持规范的准确性、促成团队对齐并降低误解风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' data-full='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 09:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47139911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47139911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Quadtrees provide efficient spatial indexing for range queries and collision detection. The interactive guide lets readers experiment with subdivision and performance trade-offs through visuals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>四叉树是一种高效的空间索引结构，适用于范围查询与碰撞检测。该互动指南通过可视化让读者直观实验分割与性能权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47207236' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://glthr.com/XML-fundamental-to-Claude' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why XML tags are so fundamental to Claude</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么 XML 标签对 Claude 至关重要</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 06:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47207236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 192</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47207236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 132</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47207236'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>XML tags give Claude a structured language to mark roles, blocks, and data in prompts, helping the model disambiguate tasks and enforce output formats. The article argues these tags are foundational for reliable prompt engineering with Claude and similar assistants. Practical guidance for building AI-powered workflows centers on leveraging structured tagging to improve consistency and interoperability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>XML 标签为 Claude 提供结构化的输入标记，以区分角色、区块与数据，从而帮助模型更好地理解任务并统一输出格式。文章认为这类标签是 Claude 等助手实现更可靠提示工程的基础，并给出在 AI 工作流中利用结构化标记提升一致性和互操作性的实践要点。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

The piece offers practical tips for starting conversations, listening actively, and reading social cues, arguing that improved communication builds opportunities and trust. Small changes can boost confidence and collaboration in everyday interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>与陌生人交谈不仅是一种社交礼仪，而是一项在工作与生活中都能带来实际收益的技能。文章给出实用建议，涵盖如何开启对话、积极倾听与解读社交线索，主张提升沟通能力能带来机会和信任。即便是小小的改变，也能显著提升自信心与协作效果。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47162059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47162059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47162059'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that predicting the next word is not genuine reasoning or general intelligence, challenging simplistic AI benchmarks and urging deeper evaluation of models.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.xda-developers.com/samsung-promised-make-old-phones-useful-galaxy-upcycle/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Samsung Upcycle Promise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三星 Upcycle 承诺：让旧设备再利用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 09:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47125263' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47125263' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47125263'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Samsung&#x27;s Upcycle Promise extends the life of aging hardware rather than discarding it, signaling a broader push toward circular economy practices. The move could offer eco-friendly paths for consumers and opens potential new revenue streams for the company.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>三星的 Upcycle 承诺旨在延长旧设备的使用寿命，推动循环经济。此举为消费者提供更环保的升级路径，也为三星带来潜在的新收入来源。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47100612' data-tags='Science,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47100612' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/viking-was-job-description-not-matter-heredity-massive-ancient-dna-study-shows' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>‘Viking’ was a job, not a matter of heredity: ancient DNA study (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>‘维京人’是职业，而非遗传决定：2020年的古DNA研究</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 21, 2026 / 05:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47100612' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47100612' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 171</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47100612'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2020 ancient DNA study concluded that being Viking was more about the job and social role than heredity, challenging myths of a fixed genetic identity. The research emphasizes how social mobility and cultural practice shaped Viking identity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2020年的古DNA研究显示，成为维京人更多是职业身份而非遗传决定，这一发现打破了将维京身份简单归因于基因的神话。研究强调社会流动性与文化实践在维京人身份形成中的作用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>就沙箱隔离策略展开细致讨论，权衡性能、兼容性和安全性保障。涵盖边界、来源证据与防止跨域泄露的技术要点，为构建安全运行时提供参考。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains why stack allocation can be faster and safer in many cases, and how compilers use escape analysis to decide stack vs. heap. Covers common pitfalls and practical tips for Go developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解释在多数情況下栈分配为何更快更安全，以及编译器如何通过逃逸分析决定放在栈还是堆。涵盖常见坑与面向 Go 开发者的实用建议。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Everett 在法官裁定录像公开后关闭 Flock 摄像头网络</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 20:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47213764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47213764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47213764'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A city shuts down its Flock camera network after a judge rules footage must be treated as public records. The move underscores the clash between surveillance infrastructure and transparency obligations, and invites questions about the costs and reliability of privatized camera networks for municipalities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在法官裁定录像应作为公共记录后，Everett 市关闭了 Flock 摄像头网络。这一举动凸显了监控基础设施与透明度义务之间的冲突，并引发关于政府采购的私有摄像头系统在成本、可靠性方面的质疑。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张务实看待 Rust，而非盲目崇拜，承认其安全性优势但强调实用性与生态成熟度的权衡。提醒把 Rust 当作工具箱中的工具，而非信仰。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://turbopuffer.com/blog/object-storage-queue' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A distributed queue in a single JSON file on object storage</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 21, 2026 / 02:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47099401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47099401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47099401'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical demonstration of a distributed queue implemented in a single JSON file stored in object storage. It explores durability, concurrency, and use cases in serverless and cost-constrained environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在对象存储中用一个单一的 JSON 文件实现分布式队列，探讨在无服务器和成本受限场景下的持久性与并发性挑战以及适用场景。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47212355' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47212355' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(221) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mandel-macaque/memento' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If AI writes code, should the session be part of the commit?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果 AI 写代码，会话应该成为提交的一部分吗？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 16:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47212355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47212355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47212355'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As AI-assisted coding becomes common, the question arises whether interactive sessions—prompts, tool outputs, and environment state—should be captured in version control. The project discusses approaches to embedding or separating session metadata from code commits to aid reproducibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着 AI 辅助写码的普及，交互会话（提示、工具输出与环境状态）是否应被记录在版本控制中成为一个问题。该项目探讨将会话元数据嵌入提交或与代码分离的做法，以提升可复现性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ec7103d0290ac896a948f304839a1e08089b0078c493e3e0330b77ded538ed6e/mandel-macaque/memento' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ec7103d0290ac896a948f304839a1e08089b0078c493e3e0330b77ded538ed6e/mandel-macaque/memento' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47178863' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47178863' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(222) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://f-droid.org/2026/02/26/board-of-directors-nominations.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>F-Droid Board of Directors nominations 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026 年 F-Droid 董事会提名</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 02:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47178863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47178863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47178863'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>F-Droid announces its 2026 board nominations, outlining how community members can participate in governance of the open-source app store. The post signals how governance choices shape project direction and sustainability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>F-Droid 公布 2026 年董事会提名，概述社区成员如何参与开源应用商店的治理。公告强调治理决策将影响项目走向和可持续性。</p>
