<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47206663' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(223) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://beabetterdev.com/2026/03/01/ai-is-making-junior-devs-useless/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is making junior devs useless</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 正在让初级开发者变得无用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 05:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47206663'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Opinion argues that AI-assisted coding erodes the demand for entry-level skills, prompting teams to rethink mentorship and onboarding. It warns of a hollow skill gap if juniors are not trained for higher-level tasks and critical thinking. The piece invites discussion about role evolution and education in software engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观点认为，人工智能辅助编码正在削弱对初级开发者技能的需求，促使团队重新思考指导与 onboarding。若对初学者缺乏更高层次任务与批判性思维的培训，可能形成空洞的技能差距。文章呼吁就软件工程中的角色演变与教育展开讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.beabetterdev.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/thumbnail111.png' data-full='https://www.beabetterdev.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/thumbnail111.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47095266'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>λProlog blends higher-order logic with logic programming, enabling more expressive rules and abstractions for program verification and language research. It’s a notable approach for researchers building meta-language tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>λProlog 将高阶逻辑与逻辑编程结合，提供更表达力的规则和抽象，适用于程序验证与语言研究。对于构建元语言工具的研究者具有重要意义。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Iowa farmers are leading a repair-rights push for farm equipment, pushing back against manufacturer lock-in and repair restrictions. The movement highlights hardware freedom trends with implications for farmer costs, equipment longevity, and open repair ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>爱荷华州农民正引领修理权倡议，抵制厂商对农机的锁定与维修限制。此运动凸显硬件自由化趋势，对农民成本、设备寿命及开放修理生态系统具有深远影响。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47114165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47114165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47114165'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An overview of the traditional Japanese death poems (jisei), exploring how poets expressed acceptance of mortality through concise, evocative lines. The piece places these poems in cultural and historical context, highlighting their influence on literature and Zen-influenced aesthetics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍日本传统的死亡诗（死亡诗句，jisei），探讨诗人如何以简短而意境深远的句子表达对死亡的接纳。文章将这些诗作放在文化与历史语境中，突出其对文学与禅宗美学的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hannahilea.com/blog/houseplant-programming/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An ode to houseplant programming (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rydberg 原子从手持无线电中探测到清晰信号</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 07:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181542' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181542' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47181542'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers show Rydberg atoms can detect clear signals from a handheld radio, enabling compact, sensitive radio sensing. This opens possibilities for lightweight receivers and new quantum-enabled sensing applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员证明 Rydberg 原子能够从手持无线电中检测到清晰信号，带来紧凑高灵敏的射频传感能力。此技术有望推动轻量接收设备与量子感测应用的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.hannahilea.com/blog/houseplant-programming/assets/thumbnail.png' data-full='https://www.hannahilea.com/blog/houseplant-programming/assets/thumbnail.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 01:18 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores a typographic mystery tied to Frank Lloyd Wright, uncovering unusual letterforms and design choices linked to his architectural language. The piece shows how typography can encode a designer&#x27;s worldview and invite cross-disciplinary inquiry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探索与 Frank Lloyd Wright 相关的字体之谜，揭示与其建筑语言相连的独特字母形态与设计选择。此探索揭示了字体如何承载设计师的世界观，并激发跨学科的思考。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47202808' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47170958' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47170958' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 175</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Onboarding modern smartphones is often painful, with data migrations, sign-ins, and ecosystem lock-in slowing setup. The post catalogs friction points and suggests pragmatic improvements—better data portability, unified sign-in, and clearer transfer tooling. Reducing friction here could improve satisfaction for device makers and carriers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现代智能手机的初始设置常常让人倍感痛苦，数据迁移、账户登录和生态体系绑定等环节拖慢了整个设置过程。文章列出主要痛点，并建议改进方向，如更好的数据可移植性、统一登录与更清晰的迁移工具。减少这些摩擦有望提升用户满意度并惠及设备制造商与运营商。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47157841' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(243) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aircada.com/blog/ai-vs-human-3d-ecommerce' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An autopsy of AI-generated 3D slop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 生成的 3D 内容质量评析</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 13:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47157841' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47157841' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47157841'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An autopsy of AI-generated 3D slop critiques the quality issues in AI-created 3D assets, examining artifacts and suggesting improvements in data, training, and evaluation for 3D workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 生成的 3D 内容质量评析揭示存在的缺陷、勘误与改进空间，呼吁在数据、训练和评估方面做出更严谨的设计。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47179038' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(244) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47179038' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: MitID, Denmark&#x27;s digital ID, was down</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>丹麦数字身份 MitID 宕机了</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 02:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47179038' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47179038' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47179038'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MitID, Denmark&#x27;s digital ID, experienced an outage, highlighting the fragility and resilience challenges of national ID systems. The incident sparked questions about redundancy, user impact, and uptime guarantees for critical digital infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>丹麦数字身份 MitID 发生宕机，暴露了国家级数字身份系统的脆弱性与韧性挑战。事件引发对冗余设计、用户影响与关键信息基础设施稳定性的讨论。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(245) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/JeffBezanson/femtolisp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>femtolisp: A lightweight, robust, scheme-like Lisp implementation</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 04:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47121539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47121539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47121539'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>femtolisp is a compact Lisp implementation inspired by Scheme, prioritizing small footprint and robust semantics for embedding or experimentation. It offers a pleasant sandbox for language enthusiasts to explore Lisp VM design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>femtolisp 是一个受 Scheme 启发的轻量 Lisp 实现，关注极小体积与稳健语义，适合嵌入式场景和语言实验。为语言爱好者提供了一个探索 Lisp 虚拟机设计的便携环境。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Øyvind Kolås shares his views on GIMP, its design philosophy, and the project’s evolving feature set. The interview sheds light on open source graphics tooling, community governance, and sustaining long-running projects in a dynamic ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Øyvind Kolås 分享了对 GIMP 的看法、设计理念以及日益发展的功能集。访谈揭示了开源图形工具、社区治理，以及在快速变化的生态中维持长期项目的挑战与策略。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(248) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mketab.org/blog/sqlite_kdbx/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why the KeePass format should be based on SQLite</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>KeePass 格式为何应基于 SQLite</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 05:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47136658' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47136658' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47136658'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocating for SQLite as KeePass&#x27;s storage backend to improve portability, indexing, and concurrency. The argument weighs simplicity and compatibility against KeePass&#x27;s existing formats and security model.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 KeePass 的存储后端改用 SQLite 可以提升可移植性、索引能力与并发性能。此论点在简化性、兼容性与 KeePass 现有格式及安全模型之间权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.usecardboard.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Cardboard (YC W26) – Agentic video editor</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 10:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47170174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47170174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cardboard positions itself as an agentic video editor, automating parts of the editing workflow with AI-like capabilities. It promises faster turnaround and smarter automation, while raising questions about reliability, creative control, and data privacy in AI-assisted video work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cardboard 将自己定位为具备代理能力的视频编辑工具，能够在编辑工作流中自动化部分任务，并具备智能化能力。它承诺更快的产出与更智能的自动化，但也引发关于可靠性、创作控制与数据隐私的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.linguabase.org/words-with-spaces.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Half million &#x27;Words with Spaces&#x27; missing from dictionaries</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>半百万个带空格的词汇未被字典收录</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 09:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47125287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47125287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47125287'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A linguistics resource notes hundreds of thousands of space-containing words are missing from dictionaries, impacting spell checkers, search, and NLP tools. The post argues for expanding lexical databases and better normalization for multilingual and hyphenated terms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份语言学资源指出有数十万带空格的词汇未被字典收录，这会影响拼写检查、搜索以及自然语言处理工具。文章呼吁扩展词库并改进多语言与带连字符词汇的正规化处理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.linguabase.org/images/linguabase-icon.png' data-full='https://www.linguabase.org/images/linguabase-icon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lapublicpress.org/2026/02/why-isnt-la-repaving-streets/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why isn&#x27;t LA repaving streets?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么洛杉矶没有重新铺路？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 08:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47154076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47154076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47154076'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece explains why LA&#x27;s street repaving has lagged, citing funding constraints, bureaucratic hurdles, and prioritization choices. It highlights the broader challenges of urban infrastructure maintenance in large cities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章解释了洛杉矶街道为何长期未铺设更新，归因于资金不足、繁琐的程序和优先级决策等因素，反映大城市基础设施维护的普遍挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lucijagregov.com/2026/02/26/the-future-of-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Future of AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工智能的未来</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 02:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47193476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47193476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47193476'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI is poised to keep expanding in capability and reach, unlocking productivity gains as models become more integrated into everyday tooling. At the same time, governance, safety, and ethical considerations will intensify, demanding clear alignment and responsible deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人工智能的未来将继续显著扩展，模型越发融入日常工具，推动生产力提升。与此同时，治理、安全与伦理议题将变得更加关键，需要明确的对齐机制和负责任的部署。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lucijagregov.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/image.png' data-full='https://lucijagregov.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lighthousesoftware.co.uk/projects/from-noise-to-image/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>From Noise to Image – interactive guide to diffusion</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>四个月的影像—视频 VAE 实验的收获</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 10:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47141107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47141107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47141107'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Four months of image-video VAE experiments reveal that optimizing for reconstruction can come at the expense of high-quality generation, and vice versa. The notes distill practical takeaways on model design, training regimes, and evaluation for image/video VAEs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>四个月的影像与视频变分自编码器实验表明，优化重建质量往往以牺牲生成质量为代价，反之亦然。笔记归纳了模型设计、训练策略与评估方法的实用要点，帮助研究者在图像/视频 VAE 任务中取舍。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探究 C++ 首次内存分配为何常见为 72 KB，揭示分配器设计、页面对齐与内存区的行为。此现象会影响启动延迟与碎片化，从而引导开发者在分配器与内存布局上的优化选择。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Just a little detail that wouldn&#x27;t sell anything discusses how tiny UX, pricing or product choices can disproportionately influence user behavior and perceived value, even when they seem marginal. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>只是一个微小的细节，不会卖出任何东西讨论了微小的用户体验、定价或产品选择如何在不显眼的地方显著影响用户行为与感知价值。文章强调在产品设计中不能忽视这些微小细节。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(263) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nand2mario.github.io/posts/2026/80386_protection/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>80386 Protection</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>80386 防护</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 07:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47138698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47138698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47138698'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Delves into the 80386’s protection features, explaining how the 32-bit protected mode, segmentation, and paging laid the groundwork for modern OS memory safety. It highlights historical challenges and practical lessons for systems programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾80386时代的保护机制，解说32位保护模式、分段和分页如何奠定现代操作系统的内存安全基础，指出历史挑战及给系统编程的启示。 这些经验对理解后续的虚拟内存和安全架构仍有借鉴意义。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47183396' data-tags='Security,Web,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47183396' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://security.googleblog.com/2026/02/cultivating-robust-and-efficient.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Robust and efficient quantum-safe HTTPS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>稳健高效的量子安全 HTTPS</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 09:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47183396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47183396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47183396'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Efforts to deploy quantum-resistant cryptography in TLS aim to future-proof web traffic without sacrificing performance. The discussion covers practical strategies, standards progress, and deployment considerations for quantum-safe HTTPS.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 TLS 中引入对量子攻击的抵御能力，力求在不牺牲性能的前提下实现量子抗性。讨论涵盖实际策略、标准进展以及量子安全 HTTPS 的部署要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEjYIUyQJtaAY06QUHFJwNJq_G2-GyovxH4kqwBEFS_WweUghi5Qkid_Mrt9Qa65XBQ254P_EMWHpRQWIV17U_oQgEyStBSHWel9v8hHlWeP4ncr9BBtfD6QUhjhPlcISln2jWWNWgf2Ol-P/s1600/googlelogo_color_200x200.png' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEjYIUyQJtaAY06QUHFJwNJq_G2-GyovxH4kqwBEFS_WweUghi5Qkid_Mrt9Qa65XBQ254P_EMWHpRQWIV17U_oQgEyStBSHWel9v8hHlWeP4ncr9BBtfD6QUhjhPlcISln2jWWNWgf2Ol-P/s1600/googlelogo_color_200x200.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47187649' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.adt2760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qt45: A small polymerase ribozyme that can synthesize itself</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qt45：一种能自我合成的聚合酶核糖酶</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 15:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47187649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47187649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47187649'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qt45 is a compact polymerase ribozyme capable of self-synthesis, a striking example of minimal biochemical self-replication. The finding fuels discussions about origins-of-life hypotheses and synthetic biology, while prompting biosafety and governance considerations for powerful self-replicating systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qt45 是一种能够自我合成的聚合酶核糖酶，展现了自我复制的极简生化能力。该发现为起源理论与合成生物学的讨论提供新材料，同时也引发对潜在自我复制系统的生物安全与治理关注。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47182659' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47182659' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(266) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/2026/02/27/lovable_app_vulnerabilities/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vibe coded Lovable-hosted app littered with basic flaws exposed 18K users</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vibe 编写的 Lovable 托管应用充斥着基础缺陷，影响 1.8 万用户</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 08:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47182659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47182659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47182659'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vibe-coded Lovable-hosted app exposed basic flaws affecting about 18,000 users, highlighting how even polished apps can overlook core security and how user trust hinges on sound security practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>由 Vibe 开发并托管的 Lovable 应用暴露了多处基础缺陷，影响大约 1.8 万用户，凸显即使看似完善的应用也可能忽视核心安全，用户信任依赖于稳健的安全实践。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(267) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.unfudged.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Unfucked - version all changes (by any tool) - local-first/source avail</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Unfucked——以任意工具记录所有变更的版本历史，本地优先，源代码可用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 13:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47172238' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 110</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47172238' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47172238'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Unfucked offers a system to version all changes across tools with a local-first design and available source. It aims to unify change history across disparate tooling, enabling offline-first workflows and open-source collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Unfucked 提供一个将跨工具的所有变更版本化的系统，采用本地优先设计并提供源代码。该项目旨在统一不同工具间的变更历史，支持离线优先的工作流和开源协作。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(268) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/flamestro/deff' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Deff – Side-by-side Git diff review in your terminal</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 09:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47169518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 109</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47169518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47169518'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Deff brings side-by-side Git diff reviewing to the terminal, streamlining code reviews and increasing readability for terminal-first workflows. It helps developers quickly compare changes without leaving the command line, boosting productivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Deff 将并排的 Git diff 审阅带到终端，简化代码评审并提升命令行工作流的可读性。开发者无需离开命令行就能快速对比改动，从而提升生产力。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47165648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47165648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Just-bash brings Bash scripting to AI agents, allowing commands and tool calls to be orchestrated from a shell. It lowers the barrier to building simple, scriptable agents and demonstrates a pragmatic approach to tool integration.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(271) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://norlinder.nu/posts/GC-Cost-CPU-vs-Memory/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dissecting the CPU-memory relationship in garbage collection (OpenJDK 26)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>分解垃圾回收中的 CPU 与内存关系（OpenJDK 26）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 05:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47137140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47137140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47137140'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A close look at how garbage collectors trade CPU work for memory efficiency in OpenJDK 26, detailing how different GC strategies affect latency, throughput, and heap pressure. The discussion helps JVM users understand tuning choices for memory-heavy applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入分析 OpenJDK 26 中垃圾回收如何以 CPU 工作换取内存效率，阐述不同 GC 策略对延迟、吞吐和堆压力的影响，帮助 JVM 用户在内存密集型应用中做出更明智的调优选择。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47144064' data-tags='Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47144064' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/02/24/paypal-stock-stripe-acquisition-report.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stripe reportedly makes offer to acquire PayPal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Stripe 或拟收购 PayPal</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 14:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47144064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47144064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47144064'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A possible Stripe bid to acquire PayPal would merge two large payments ecosystems, potentially reshaping consumer wallets, merchant onboarding, and cross-border rails. The move could invite regulatory scrutiny and spur strategic shifts in fintech competition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据传 Stripe 正寻求收购 PayPal，这将整合两大支付生态系统，可能重塑消费者钱包、商家接入和跨境支付网络。此举可能引发监管关注，并推动金融科技领域的战略调整。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47189853' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/openai-reaches-deal-deploy-ai-models-us-department-war-classified-network-2026-02-28/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI reaches deal to deploy AI models on U.S. DoW classified network</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 就在美国战争部机密网络部署 AI 模型达成协议</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 19:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47189853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47189853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47189853'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI clinches a deal to deploy AI models inside the Department of War&#x27;s classified network, signaling deeper government use of advanced AI with secure integrations. The arrangement highlights tensions between rapid AI adoption and governance, oversight, and cybersecurity requirements. It could set a precedent for other agencies and vendors negotiating access to restricted networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 与战争部在机密网络中部署 AI 模型达成协议，标志着对先进 AI 的政府级别更深度应用与安全集成。该安排凸显快速采用 AI 与治理、监管及网络安全要求之间的张力，也可能为其他机构和厂商就进入受限网络的谈判设定先例。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 16:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47212576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47212576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47212576'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Timber leverages Ollama to run classical ML models, claiming a remarkable 336x speedup over Python-based runtimes. This approach highlights how local, lean inference pipelines can outperform traditional Python stacks for certain workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Timber 借助 Ollama 运行经典机器学习模型，声称相比 Python 运行时实现了高达 336 倍的加速。这一思路凸显了本地、精简推理流水线在某些工作负载上的潜在领先性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47189625' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(279) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://a-cup-of.coffee/blog/ostree-bootc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bootc and OSTree: Modernizing Linux System Deployment</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bootc 与 OSTree：现代化 Linux 系统部署</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 18:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47189625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47189625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47189625'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OSTree enables atomic, versioned system deployments, and Bootc offers a streamlined boot process to tie deployments together. The combination simplifies updates, rollbacks, and reproducibility on Linux devices, with particular appeal for edge and embedded systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OSTree 实现原子性、逐版本的系统部署，Bootc 为引导流程提供简化的整合。二者结合使 Linux 设备的更新、回滚与可重复性变得更简单，特别适用于边缘与嵌入式系统。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://a-cup-of.coffee/blog/ostree-bootc/cover.png' data-full='https://a-cup-of.coffee/blog/ostree-bootc/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47151784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47151784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Clocksimulator.com offers a distraction-free analog clock UI that focuses on minimalism and performance. It demonstrates how a tiny, focused web app can deliver a pleasant, distraction-free utility without feature bloat.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Clocksimulator.com 提供一个极简、无干扰的模拟时钟界面，强调设计简洁和响应速度。这个项目展示了一个小而专注的网页应用如何在不堆叠功能的情况下提供高质量的实用体验。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47147609' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47147609' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(281) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turing Completeness of GNU find</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GNU find 的图灵完备性</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 21:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47147609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47147609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47147609'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study argues that GNU find, via its expression language and -exec capabilities, can simulate a Turing machine, illustrating surprising computational power in utility programs. This challenges assumptions about what counts as a programming language and has implications for esolangs and scriptability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明 GNU find 通过表达式语言和 -exec 等特性，具备模拟图灵机的能力，揭示了实用工具的意外计算能力。这对把何者算作编程语言的认知提出挑战，并影响对极简语言和可脚本性的理解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47150674' data-tags='Design,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47150674' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(282) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://paultendo.github.io/posts/confusable-vision-visual-similarity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I rendered 1,418 confusables over 230 fonts. Most aren&#x27;t confusable to the eye</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在 230 种字体中渲染了 1,418 个混淆符号：大多数并不肉眼混淆</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 04:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47150674' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47150674' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47150674'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study visualizing glyph confusability across 230 fonts found that most potential confusions are not visually obvious to the eye, challenging assumptions in font design and OCR. The results underscore the complexity of visual similarity beyond simple glyph shapes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项对230种字体中的1418个潜在混淆字符的可视化分析显示，大多数并不会在肉眼上产生直观混淆，挑战了字体设计和光学字符识别中的直观假设。这表明视觉相似性远比简单字形要复杂。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://paultendo.github.io/og/confusable-vision-visual-similarity.png' data-full='https://paultendo.github.io/og/confusable-vision-visual-similarity.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(283) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.feld.me/posts/2026/02/using-new-bridges-freebsd-15/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using the new bridges of FreeBSD 15</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 FreeBSD 15 的新桥接功能</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 14:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47115575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47115575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47115575'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FreeBSD 15&#x27;s new bridging features enable simpler, more powerful network setups, with practical notes on configuring bridges, performance considerations, and potential use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FreeBSD 15 的新桥接功能让网络配置更简单、强大，文章给出实际的配置要点、性能考量与潜在应用场景。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47209788' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47209788' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(284) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://maurycyz.com/misc/c_files/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why does C have the best file API</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何 C 拥有最佳的文件 API</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 11:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47209788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47209788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47209788'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>C’s file API offers low-level control, portability, and predictable behavior that shine in systems programming. The piece argues these attributes persist as advantages despite modern higher-level alternatives, with direct OS interaction and a simple, consistent interface. The trade-off is safety and ergonomics, but for certain domains, the API remains unmatched.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>C 的文件 I/O 原语提供了底层控制、可移植性和可预测性，这使其在系统编程中仍然出色。文章认为尽管出现了更高层次的替代方案，这些特性仍然是其优势，原因在于直接的操作系统交互和简单统一的接口。代价是安全性和易用性，但在特定领域，C 的 API 仍然无人能及。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47139690' data-tags='Privacy,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47139690' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(285) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.9news.com/article/news/local/denver-removing-flock-cameras-new-axon-contract/73-640b5af3-7c87-4fea-8aa1-2510ad3257b8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Denver dumps Flock, awards contract to Axon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>丹佛取消 Flock，改签 Axon 合同</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 09:16 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Parakeet.cpp 是一个用纯 C++ 编写、借助 Apple Metal GPU 加速的语音识别推理引擎，支持在设备端实现更快的识别。它强调了性能特性、API 设计以及对 iOS/macOS 开发者的潜在应用场景。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47121864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47121864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>VTT&#x27;s donut-shaped lab battery reaches 80% charge in under 10 minutes, signaling progress in fast charging tech. The result highlights potential gains for EV charging and battery design, while noting scale-up and efficiency questions.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(290) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/FreHu/vscode-fresh-file-explorer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fresh File Explorer – VS Code extension for navigating recent work</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 10:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113325' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113325' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47113325'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fresh File Explorer is a VS Code extension designed to speed up navigation by exposing recent files and work sessions in an accessible panel. It reduces context-switching and helps developers jump back into work faster. The extension demonstrates a shift toward more ergonomic, task-focused tooling inside editors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fresh File Explorer 是一个 VS Code 扩展，旨在通过展示最近的文件和工作会话来提升导航效率。它可以减少上下文切换，帮助开发者更快回到正在做的事。这一扩展体现了编辑器内更注重任务导向的工具设计趋势。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47167432' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47167432' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(291) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.morling.dev/blog/hardwood-new-parser-for-apache-parquet/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hardwood: A New Parser for Apache Parquet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hardwood：Apache Parquet 的全新解析器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 07:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47167432' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47167432' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47167432'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hardwood introduces a new parser for Apache Parquet designed to improve parsing speed and resilience relative to existing implementations. It focuses on robust handling of the Parquet metadata and nested schemas, with potential for streaming reads and better ergonomics for developers integrating Parquet data into pipelines. This could influence data tooling performance in analytics stacks that rely on Parquet.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hardwood 为 Apache Parquet 提供了一种全新的解析器，目标是提升解析速度与容错能力。它强调对 Parquet 元数据和嵌套模式的稳健处理，并可能支持流式读取，方便将 Parquet 数据接入数据管线。此举可能提升分析栈中 Parquet 相关工具的性能与可用性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.morling.dev/images/gunnar_morling.jpg' data-full='https://www.morling.dev/images/gunnar_morling.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47104227' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47104227' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(292) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ta.fo/aesthetics-of-single-threading/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Aesthetics of single threading</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>单线程的美学</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 21, 2026 / 12:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47104227' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47104227' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47104227'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Single-threaded design is advocated for its predictability and simplicity in certain workloads, resisting the lure of ever-more complex concurrency. The piece argues that elegance can reside in serial execution when it matches the problem domain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>单线程设计在某些工作负载下因可预测性与简洁性而受青睐，避免过度追求并发带来的复杂性。文章认为，串行执行在匹配问题域时也能展现优雅。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.studiohaze.co.kr/logos/tafo-journal-logo.png' data-full='https://www.studiohaze.co.kr/logos/tafo-journal-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47137199' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47137199' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(293) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.oranlooney.com/post/grifters-skeptics-marks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Modeling cycles of grift with evolutionary game theory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用进化博弈论建模骗局循环</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 05:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47137199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47137199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47137199'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Using evolutionary game theory, the post models cycles of grifters, skeptics, and marks to show why scams persist and how defenders might disrupt them. It highlights dynamics of trust, deception, and countermeasures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过进化博弈论建模诈骗者、怀疑者与受害者之间的互动周期，揭示骗局为何持续存在以及防御方如何打破循环，强调信任、欺骗与对策的动态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.oranlooney.com/post/grifters-skeptics-marks_files/lead.jpg' data-full='https://www.oranlooney.com/post/grifters-skeptics-marks_files/lead.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47211830' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(294) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/AlexsJones/llmfit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Right-sizes LLM models to your system&#x27;s RAM, CPU, and GPU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把大语言模型按系统 RAM、CPU 与 GPU 的容量进行合适调优</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 15:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47211830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47211830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47211830'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical approach to tailoring large language models to available hardware using tools like llmfit. It helps users fit models to memory and compute constraints, enabling more affordable local inference.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供一种利用 llmfit 等工具将大语言模型按可用硬件进行尺寸自适应的实用方法，帮助在受限的内存和算力条件下实现更经济的本地推理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4f8526ab2d9245c591f5fe777e27c90faad8197267f4def7de8f332ceffda933/AlexsJones/llmfit' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4f8526ab2d9245c591f5fe777e27c90faad8197267f4def7de8f332ceffda933/AlexsJones/llmfit' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47165230' data-tags='Data,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47165230' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(295) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vondra.me/posts/the-real-cost-of-random-io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The real cost of random I/O</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>随机 I/O 的真实成本</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 04:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47165230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47165230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47165230'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Random I/O incurs far higher latency than sequential access due to seeks and cache behavior. The analysis shows how random patterns translate into higher CPU and I/O costs, influencing database design and storage expectations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随机 I/O 相比顺序访问带来更高的延迟，原因在于寻道和缓存行为。分析显示随机模式如何转化为更高的 CPU 与 I/O 成本，影响数据库设计与存储预期。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vondra.me/avatar.png' data-full='https://vondra.me/avatar.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47124454' data-tags='Culture,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47124454' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(296) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openculture.com/2026/02/an-introduction-to-the-codex-seraphinianus.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An Introduction to the Codex Seraphinianus, the Strangest Book Ever Published</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《塞拉菲尼亚努斯手抄本》：有史以来最怪异的书籍导读</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 08:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47124454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47124454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47124454'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Codex Seraphinianus is a 20th-century illustrated encyclopedia written in an invented script with surreal drawings. This introduction covers its creation, structure, and enduring mystique, illustrating how an unreadable text can inspire art, curiosity, and design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《塞拉菲尼亚努斯手抄本》是二十世纪以虚构文字创作的超现实百科全书，配有离奇插图。本文介绍其创作背景、结构与持续的神秘感，展示未解读文本如何激发艺术、好奇心与设计灵感。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn8.openculture.com/2026/02/22224849/codex2-2.png' data-full='https://cdn8.openculture.com/2026/02/22224849/codex2-2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47077215' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47077215' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(297) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://matklad.github.io/2026/02/14/justifying-text-wrap-pretty.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Justifying Text-Wrap: Pretty</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>文本换行对齐：美观排版</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 19, 2026 / 10:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47077215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47077215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47077215'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Justifying text wrap explores how layout decisions affect readability and aesthetics beyond pure line-breaking. It argues for careful justification strategies—balancing ragged edges, hyphenation, and column width to produce pleasing, legible text on screens and print.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文本换行的对齐设计从可读性与美观出发，讨论如何在行断、连字符和列宽之间取得平衡。对屏幕和印刷排版中的文本美感提供了实践性建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/2/27/Gutenberg_bible_Old_Testament_Epistle_of_St_Jerome.jpg' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/2/27/Gutenberg_bible_Old_Testament_Epistle_of_St_Jerome.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基准测试比较了 Go 的多种并发哈希映射实现，揭示了速度、内存和实现复杂度之间的权衡。为开源库作者与高吞吐应用选择合适的数据结构提供参考。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(300) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2013/06/director-national-intelligences-word-games-explained-how-government-deceived' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How the Government Deceived Congress in the Debate over Surveillance Powers (2013)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>政府在2013年监控权限辩论中对国会的误导</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 12:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47210572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47210572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47210572'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on how intelligence leadership used selective language to sway congressional debates over surveillance powers in 2013. The piece exposes misstatements and framing tactics that shaped policy outcomes. It underscores the need for precise, truth-driven oversight in national security discussions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾2013年的监控权限辩论中，情报机构如何通过选择性用语来左右国会意见。文章揭露了误导性陈述与话术策略如何影响政策走向，并强调在国家安全议题中实现精准、以事实为基础的监督的必要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thenewstack.io/red-hat-enters-the-cloud-native-developer-desktop-market/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Red Hat takes on Docker Desktop with its enterprise Podman Desktop build</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Red Hat 用企业版 Podman Desktop 与 Docker Desktop 竞争</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 05:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47151163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47151163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47151163'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Red Hat unveils enterprise Podman Desktop, positioning it as a Docker Desktop alternative with integrated enterprise features, Kubernetes support, and a focus on rootless containers. The move signals a shift toward more secure, daemonless workflows in enterprise dev environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Red Hat 推出企业版 Podman Desktop，定位为 Docker Desktop 的替代方案，集成企业级特性、Kubernetes 支持，并聚焦于无守护进程的容器工作流。这一举动预示着企业开发环境将朝更安全、无守护进程的方向发展。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47152448' data-tags='Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47152448' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(303) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-10114-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sub-second volumetric 3D printing by synthesis of holographic light fields</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚秒级体积三维打印：通过全息光场合成实现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 07:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47152448' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47152448' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47152448'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate sub-second volumetric 3D printing by synthesizing holographic light fields that encode an entire object in a single exposure. By projecting a carefully designed holographic light field, the resin cures in three dimensions without layer-by-layer scanning, enabling ultra-fast fabrication of complex geometries. The method hinges on precise light-field control and rapid image synthesis hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究通过全息光场的合成来实现亚秒级的体积打印，将整个三维对象在单次曝光中编码到光场中。避免传统逐层打印的过程，利用高精度光场控制实现复杂几何体的超快速制造。该技术若实现规模化的材料和硬件兼容性，可能彻底改变快速原型制作与按需制造的速度与灵活性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41586-026-10114-5/MediaObjects/41586_2026_10114_Fig1_HTML.png' data-full='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41586-026-10114-5/MediaObjects/41586_2026_10114_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47182387' data-tags='Show HN,Open Source,Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47182387' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(304) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/hjtenklooster/claude-file-recovery' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Claude-File-Recovery, recover files from your ~/.claude sessions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 文件恢复工具，能从 ~/.claude 会话中恢复文件</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 08:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47182387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47182387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47182387'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude-File-Recovery is an open-source tool that extracts and recovers files from your ~/.claude sessions, helping you salvage data if a session drops, is interrupted, or if you need to retrieve drafts. It demonstrates a practical approach to user data resilience in AI workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude-File-Recovery 是一个开源工具，能从你的 ~/.claude 会话中提取并恢复文件，帮助在会话中断或意外丢失时找回草稿数据。它展示了在 AI 工作流中提升数据韧性的切实做法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e0e29ef26268fcc1338b4f67e7f9b2ba981400972a7d068684f1fd34315d0e77/hjtenklooster/claude-file-recovery' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e0e29ef26268fcc1338b4f67e7f9b2ba981400972a7d068684f1fd34315d0e77/hjtenklooster/claude-file-recovery' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47139519' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47139519' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(305) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://linkinghub.elsevier.com/retrieve/pii/S000169182600171X' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cardiorespiratory fitness is associated with lower anger and anxiety</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>心肺耐力与较低的愤怒和焦虑相关</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 09:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47139519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47139519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47139519'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds higher cardiorespiratory fitness is associated with lower anger and anxiety. The result adds to evidence that physical conditioning supports mental health and implications for treatment and public health messaging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现，心肺耐力水平越高，愤怒与焦虑越低。这为体能训练对心理健康的积极作用提供了证据，可能影响治疗建议和公共卫生宣传。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(315) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Optophone' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Optophone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Optophone（光声读屏器）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 20, 2026 / 13:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47093978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47093978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47093978'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Optophone was an early assistive technology that converts printed text into musical tones for blind readers, foreshadowing later tactile and speech-based interfaces. The device illustrates how inventors used sound to bridge gaps between vision and information.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Optophone 是一种早期辅助技术，通过将印刷文本转化为音调帮助盲人阅读，为后来的触觉与语音界面铺路，体现了用声音连接视觉信息的探索。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>更多牛群、更多妻子</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 07:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47152967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47152967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47152967'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative look at how livestock wealth—cows—may influence marriage markets and social structures in different societies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文以牲畜财富为切入点，探讨牛等资源如何影响婚娶市场与社会结构。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>灯塔：极端孤立如何改变身体与大脑</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 20, 2026 / 05:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47087838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47087838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47087838'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Lighthouse article reviews how extreme isolation affects the body and mind, with implications for space missions, polar expeditions, and remote work. It highlights changes in sleep, stress responses, and cognitive function under prolonged confinement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨极端孤立对身体和大脑的影响，涉及太空任务、极地探险及远程工作的启示。长期封闭环境下的睡眠、压力反应与认知功能变化被详细揭示。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47178758'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article reports the successful synthesis of an aromatic five-membered silicon ring, a milestone in inorganic chemistry. Such structures challenge conventional silicon chemistry, opening doors to novel materials and bonding concepts with potential implications for electronics and catalysis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道了芳香性五元硅环的成功合成，这是无机化学领域的里程碑之作。这些结构挑战了传统的硅化学，可能为新材料、电子学和催化等领域带来新的结合与应用前景。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(325) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.origami-robotics.com/blog/dexterity-deadlocks.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Robotic Dexterity Deadlock</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>机器人灵巧性的僵局</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 11:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47184744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47184744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47184744'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Robotic Dexterity Deadlock argues that improving manipulation hinges on solving perception, planning, and actuation bottlenecks, not just advanced hardware. The piece maps why achieving human-like dexterity remains elusive and outlines potential paths forward for researchers and engineers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《机器人灵巧性的僵局》指出，提升操作灵活性不仅要依赖先进的硬件，更要解决感知、规划与执行等瓶颈。文章梳理了为何实现类似人类的灵巧度仍然困难，并为研究者和工程师指明了可能的突破方向。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 02:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47149582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47149582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47149582'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Debian has removed Free Pascal Compiler and Lazarus IDE from its repositories, citing maintenance and licensing concerns. The move impacts Pascal developers and the Lazarus community, who may need to seek alternatives or contribute back.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Debian 已将 Free Pascal 编译器与 Lazarus IDE 从仓库中移除，原因是维护与许可方面的考虑。这一举动影响到使用 Pascal 的开发者与 Lazarus 社区，可能需要寻找替代方案或贡献回馈。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47114054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47114054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Tears of Donald Knuth is an opinion piece reflecting on the emotional dimensions of long-running contributions to CS and typography, using Knuth as a touchstone.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇观点文章以 Knuth 的情感表达为线索，讨论科技领域长期贡献者在面对挑战时的情感与坚持。</p>
