<p class='hn-title'>(337) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.amzi.com/articles/irq_expert_system.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Extending C with Prolog (1994)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Prolog 扩展 C（1994）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 08:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47112148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47112148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47112148'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 1994 piece shows merging C with Prolog to build expert systems, blending imperative performance with logic programming. It reflects early attempts at multi-paradigm design in systems programming and foreshadows later approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1994 年的介绍展示了用 Prolog 来扩展 C，结合命令式的执行效率与逻辑编程的推理能力来构建专家系统。这体现了早期的多范式编程思路，并预示了后来的混合语言与系统架构趋势。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47110384' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47110384' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(338) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/atgreen/cl-kawa' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cl-kawa: Scheme on Java on Common Lisp</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>cl-kawa：用 Common Lisp 在 Java 平台上实现 Scheme</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 04:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47110384' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47110384' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47110384'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cl-kawa implements Scheme on the JVM using Common Lisp, enabling Scheme code to run alongside Java with interop to Java libraries. The project highlights the challenges of hosting a Lisp dialect on the Java platform and offers a path for language experimentation on the JVM.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>cl-kawa 让 Scheme 能在 JVM 上运行，借助 Common Lisp 的实现实现与 Java 的互操作。该工作揭示在 JVM 上混合 Lisp 方言的挑战与可能性，为语言实验和跨语言开发提供了有趣的路径。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 09:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47182907' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47182907' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers show Rydberg atoms can detect clear signals from a handheld radio, enabling compact, sensitive radio sensing. This opens possibilities for lightweight receivers and new quantum-enabled sensing applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员证明 Rydberg 原子能够从手持无线电中检测到清晰信号，带来紧凑高灵敏的射频传感能力。此技术有望推动轻量接收设备与量子感测应用的发展。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47159833' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47159833' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47159833'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Steerling/ concept algebra offers a framework to steer language models toward interpretable behaviors, exploring algebraic constructs to guide outputs. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用概念代数引导可解释语言模型，提供一种让模型朝向可理解行为的框架，探索代数结构来控制输出。这一方法有望提升AI系统的可控性与透明度，并降低不安全或意外输出的风险。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(349) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/pinterest-is-drowning-in-a-sea-of-ai-slop-and-auto-moderation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pinterest is drowning in a sea of AI slop and auto-moderation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pinterest 面临 AI 内容泛滥与自动审核挑战</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 20:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47117966' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47117966' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47117966'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pinterest struggles with a flood of AI-generated content and the burden of auto-moderation, raising questions about content quality, moderation tools, and user experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pinterest 正在应对 AI 生成内容泛滥和自动审核带来的质控与工作量问题，影响平台内容质量与用户体验。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(350) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deadlime.hu/en/2026/02/22/computer-generated-dream-world/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Computer-generated dream world: Virtual reality for a 286 processor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>286 处理器上的计算机生成梦境世界：虚拟现实</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 20:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47213866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47213866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47213866'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful exploration of virtual reality running on an 80286-class processor demonstrates how constrained hardware can still deliver immersive experiences. It&#x27;s a reminder that the bottlenecks of old tech can inspire new approaches to efficiency and performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 80286 级别的处理器上实现虚拟现实，展示了受限硬件仍能提供沉浸式体验的可能性。通过一系列渲染与输入的巧妙技巧，作品把复古硬件推向极限。它提醒人们，旧技术的瓶颈也能激发更高效的设计思路。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(351) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://laughingmeme.org/2026/02/09/code-has-always-been-the-easy-part.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Code has always been the easy part</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编码一直是最简单的部分</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 21, 2026 / 02:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47099476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47099476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47099476'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that the real challenges lie beyond writing code—requirements, collaboration, and system design shape outcomes more than syntax. It urges engineers to invest in processes, communication, and product sense to deliver durable software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张真正的挑战不在编写代码本身，而在需求、协作和系统设计等因素。呼吁工程师投入于流程、沟通和产品意识，以交付更稳健的软件。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(352) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 26-Gram Butterfly-Inspired Robot Achieving Autonomous Tailless Flight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>26克蝴蝶式机器人实现自主无尾飞行</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 06:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47111164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47111164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47111164'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 26-gram butterfly-inspired robot demonstrates autonomous tailless flight, using lightweight actuation and flexible wings, a step forward for micro aerial robotics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>26 克蝴蝶灵感的机器人实现了自主无尾飞行，依靠轻量化驱动与柔性翼面，标志着微型无人机领域的一步重要进展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(353) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pcbtracer.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PCB Tracer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PCB Tracer</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 01:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47135009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47135009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47135009'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PCB Tracer offers a browser-based tool to trace nets and inspect PCB designs, making it easier for hobbyists and professionals to understand complex schematics and verify layouts. The project lowers the barrier to debugging and iteration in PCB work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PCB Tracer 提供基于浏览器的电路板网线追踪与设计检查工具，方便爱好者和专业人士理解复杂原理图并核对布局。该工具降低了PCB调试与迭代的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pcbtracer.com/images/PCB_Tracer_Image.png' data-full='https://pcbtracer.com/images/PCB_Tracer_Image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(354) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://text.npr.org/nx-s1-5719338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Large study finds link between cannabis use in teens and psychosis later</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型研究发现青少年大麻使用与后期精神病风险相关</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 08:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47124877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47124877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47124877'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A longitudinal study links teen cannabis use with higher risk of psychosis later, with effects depending on frequency and age of use. The findings contribute to debates on policy and adolescent health.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纵向研究发现青少年大麻使用与 later 概念的精神病风险增加相关，频率和使用年龄影响强度。这一结果为政策和青少年健康辩论提供了证据。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(355) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://coderush.me/hydroph0bia-part3/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hydroph0bia – fixed SecureBoot bypass for UEFI firmware from Insyde H2O (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hydroph0bia：对 Insyde H2O UEFI 固件的 SecureBoot 绕过漏洞修复（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 14:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47172730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47172730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47172730'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Details a fixed SecureBoot bypass in Insyde H2O UEFI firmware, illustrating ongoing firmware security challenges and how vendors patch critical boot-time vulnerabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍对 Insyde H2O UEFI 固件 SecureBoot 绕过漏洞的修复，揭示固件安全领域的持续挑战以及厂商对关键引导阶段漏洞的修补实践。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47187121' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(356) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/wkoszek/emuko' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Emuko: Fast RISC-V emulator written in Rust, boots Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Emuko：用 Rust 编写的快速 RISC-V 模拟器，能够启动 Linux</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 15:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47187121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47187121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47187121'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Emuko is a high-performance RISC-V emulator written in Rust that can boot a Linux kernel, a notable achievement for systems emulation in safe languages. It provides a practical platform for experimenting with OS internals and virtualization ideas without the overhead of full hardware. The project showcases Rust’s viability for low-level tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Emuko 是用 Rust 编写的高性能 RISC-V 模拟器，能够引导 Linux 内核，是在安全语言中实现系统仿真的重要成就。它为研究操作系统内部结构和虚拟化思路提供了一个实际平台，避免了直接硬件的开销。该项目展示了 Rust 作为底层工具的可行性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ab0acc14d220da5153f27734abc89d1d84c88c1cb0f1f8d23f4257c8bf2ef26a/wkoszek/emuko' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ab0acc14d220da5153f27734abc89d1d84c88c1cb0f1f8d23f4257c8bf2ef26a/wkoszek/emuko' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(357) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://dionisiydk.blogspot.com/2026/02/bpatterns-rewrite-engine-with-smalltalk.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Working on Pharo Smalltalk: BPatterns: Rewrite Engine with Smalltalk Style</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 01:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47178344' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47178344' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47178344'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at BPatterns, a rewrite engine built in the Pharo Smalltalk ecosystem, showcasing pattern-based rewrites and idiomatic Smalltalk design. The post demonstrates how language-integrated tooling can simplify complex transformations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在 Pharo Smalltalk 生态系统中实现的 BPatterns 重写引擎，展示基于模式的重写和地道的 Smalltalk 设计。该文展示了语言集成工具如何简化复杂转换。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(358) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/quailyquaily/aqua' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Aqua: A CLI message tool for AI agents</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 18:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47117169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47117169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47117169'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Aqua provides a CLI to interact with AI agents, enabling scripted prompts, messaging, and orchestration of agent workflows. It aims to streamline multi-agent experimentation and automation for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Aqua 提供一个命令行界面，用于与 AI 代理进行交互、发送消息并编排代理工作流。设计目标是加速多代理实验与自动化。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47179553' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(359) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://postmarketos.org/blog/2026/02/26/pmOS-update-2026-02/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PostmarketOS in 2026-02: generic kernels, bans use of generative AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PostmarketOS 2026-02 更新：通用内核与禁止使用生成式 AI</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 04:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47179553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47179553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47179553'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PostmarketOS announces a February 2026 update focused on generic kernels to widen device support, along with a policy banning generative AI usage due to safety/licensing concerns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PostmarketOS 发布 2026 年 2 月更新，聚焦通用内核以扩大设备支持，同时禁止使用生成式 AI，出于安全与许可方面的考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://postmarketos.org/static/img/2026-02/monthly.jpg' data-full='https://postmarketos.org/static/img/2026-02/monthly.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47206082' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Open Source,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47206082' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(360) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/cosmiciron/vmprint' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I built a zero-browser, pure-JS typesetting engine for bit-perfect PDFs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：零浏览器、纯 JavaScript 的位精确排版引擎</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 04:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47206082'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A pure-JS, zero-dependency typesetting engine that outputs bit-perfect PDFs without relying on a browser&#x27;s layout engine. It demonstrates how much you can push in the JS sandbox and opens up offline, deterministic printing workflows. This could inform reproducible publishing pipelines and minimal runtime footprints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个纯 JavaScript、零依赖的排版引擎，输出位精确的 PDF，不依赖浏览器排版引擎。展示了在纯 JS 沙箱内还能实现的极致排版能力，并为离线、确定性打印工作流打开了可能性。这样的实现可能推动可重复出版流程和最小化运行时开销的探索。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LLMs 的确定性编程</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 14:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47158834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47158834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47158834'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues for making LLM-driven programs deterministic through controlled prompting, tool usage, and state management. This improves reproducibility, testability, and safety when deploying AI-powered software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出通过受控提示、工具调用和状态管理等手段，让基于 LLM 的程序实现确定性。提升了在部署 AI 驱动软件时的可重复性、可测试性和安全性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(362) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prql-lang.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pipelined Relational Query Language, Pronounced &quot;Prequel&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>管线化关系查询语言，发音为“Prequel”</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 21, 2026 / 04:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47099980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47099980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47099980'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PRQL offers a pipeline-style query language that compiles to SQL, aiming to improve readability and composability of data queries. By enabling incremental abstractions and integration with existing databases, it could reshape analytics workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PRQL 提供一种管线化查询语言，可编译为 SQL，提升查询的可读性与组合性，并便于与现有数据库对接，可能改变数据分析管线的构建方式。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新一代航天器正压垮通信网络</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 08:21 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47182339'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As spacecraft become data-intensive, current communication networks struggle with bandwidth, latency, and reliability. The piece argues for redesigned space communication architectures and edge processing to meet mission demands.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着航天器数据密集化，现有通信网络在带宽、时延和可靠性方面面临挑战。文章主张对航天通信体系进行重新设计，并强化边缘处理以满足任务需求。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(366) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/AI-fluency-index' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic Education the AI Fluency Index</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 发布 AI 流畅度指数教育版</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 07:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47123590' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47123590' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47123590'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic&#x27;s AI Fluency Index measures how well people can understand and interact with AI systems. The index has implications for education, policy, and workforce readiness as AI tools become more embedded in work and life. It signals a push to quantify AI literacy beyond basic exposure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 的 AI 流畅度指数衡量人们理解和使用 AI 系统的能力。该指数关系到教育、政策和职场适应性，随着 AI 工具融入日常工作与生活，它强调不仅是接触，还需具备深入的 AI 素养。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾到2010年的科技愿景，评估AI、移动、网络与硬件领域的预测落地情况，揭示哪些已成真、哪些未实现。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47180140' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47180140' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(368) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.16398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chorba: A novel CRC32 implementation (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chorba：一种新颖的 CRC32 实现（2024）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 05:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47180140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47180140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47180140'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chorba introduces a novel CRC32 implementation that aims to boost throughput and reduce memory usage. The approach may employ table-free algorithms or processor-specific optimizations to speed up checksums on large data streams. Efficient CRC implementations matter for data integrity in storage, networking, and embedded systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chorba 提出了一种新颖的 CRC32 实现，目标是提升吞吐量并降低内存占用。该方法可能采用无表算法或专用处理器优化，以在大数据流中加速校验和计算。高效的 CRC 实现对存储、网络和嵌入式系统的数据完整性至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(371) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/shiehn/sas-audio-processor' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Audio Toolkit for Agents</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47207806' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47207806' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47207806'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN presents an audio toolkit for agents—modular components for audio input/output, speech processing, and streaming pipelines. It demonstrates how agents can reason over real-time audio data and integrate with external services to enhance interactive workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了一个面向代理的音频工具包，提供音频输入/输出、语音处理和流式处理等模块化组件。展示了代理如何基于实时音频数据进行推理并接入外部服务，提升交互工作流的能力。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 AMD Ryzen AI Max+ 集群上本地运行一万亿参数的 LLM</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 17:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47202614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47202614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47202614'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AMD demonstrates locally running a trillion-parameter LLM on a Ryzen AI Max+ cluster, likely using model parallelism and quantization to fit hardware constraints. This signals growing feasibility of private, on-prem AI at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AMD 展示在 Ryzen AI Max+ 集群上本地运行万亿参数的 LLM，凭借模型并行和量化等技术实现硬件约束下的可行性。这表明在私有、本地大规模 AI 部署方面的可行性正在提升。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47097446' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47097446' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(373) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wiki.freecad.org/Topological_naming_problem' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Topological Naming Problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>拓扑命名问题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 20, 2026 / 20:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47097446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47097446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47097446'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Topological naming problem explains why FreeCAD&#x27;s modeled objects sometimes get unstable or non-deterministic identifiers due to topology changes, complicating references and data interchange. The issue motivates design approaches to stabilize names and serialization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>拓扑命名问题解释了 FreeCAD 的建模对象在拓扑发生变化时可能获得不稳定或非确定的标识符，影响引用与数据交换。此问题推动在命名与序列化方面的稳健性设计改进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wiki.freecad.org/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=1.25.0' data-full='https://wiki.freecad.org/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=1.25.0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47201816' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47201816' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(377) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/jonwiggins/xmloxide' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Xmloxide – an agent made rust replacement for libxml2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Xmloxide：用 Rust 重写的 libxml2 替代方案</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 15:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47201816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47201816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47201816'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Xmloxide is a Rust-based XML parser intended to replace libxml2 with safer memory handling and improved performance. It exemplifies a broader migration from C libraries to Rust to improve security and reliability in core tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Xmloxide 是一个用 Rust 实现的 XML 解析器，旨在更安全地取代 libxml2 并提升性能。它体现了将关键 C 库迁移到 Rust 以提高安全性与可靠性的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c99624e41dc846e59cde9296619b2d43392c23ae6efb40576aba783a536d6baa/jonwiggins/xmloxide' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c99624e41dc846e59cde9296619b2d43392c23ae6efb40576aba783a536d6baa/jonwiggins/xmloxide' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47121716' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47121716' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(378) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://paultendo.github.io/posts/unicode-confusables-nfkc-conflict/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Confusables.txt and NFKC disagree on 31 characters</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Confusables.txt 与 NFKC 在31个字符上的不一致</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 04:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47121716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47121716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47121716'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Unicode studies show Confusables.txt mappings clash with NFKC normalization for 31 characters, producing potential security and UX pitfalls in input handling and font rendering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示 Confusables.txt 的映射与 NFKC 正规化在 31 个字符上存在冲突，可能带来输入处理与字体渲染的安全与用户体验问题。文章给出具体示例，并倡议在依赖 Unicode 清理的应用中谨慎选择正规化策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://paultendo.github.io/og/unicode-confusables-nfkc-conflict.png' data-full='https://paultendo.github.io/og/unicode-confusables-nfkc-conflict.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://journals.plos.org/plosgenetics/article?id=10.1371/journal.pgen.1012002' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Genetic underpinnings of chills from art and music</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>艺术与音乐引起寒颤的遗传机制</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 22:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47118838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47118838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47118838'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study maps genetic factors that influence how art and music induce chills, linking neuroscience with aesthetics. The findings help explain why certain sensory experiences evoke strong emotional responses.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47076789' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(382) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terezi.pyrope.net/ccgoto/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Emulating Goto in Scheme with Continuations</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Centipawn advantage is a unit used by chess engines to quantify how far the position is from equality. The article explains interpretation, how it translates to practical gains, and its limits when the position is highly complex.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>百步棋分是象棋引擎用来量化局面领先程度的单位，能帮助理解评估值。文章解释了该单位的解读、对实际优势的转化，以及在复杂局面中的局限性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(384) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.collabora.com/news-and-blog/news-and-events/rk3588-and-rk3576-video-decoders-support-merged-in-the-upstream-linux-kernel.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RK3588 and RK3576 video decoders support merged in the upstream Linux Kernel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RK3588 与 RK3576 视频解码器在上游 Linux 内核中合并支持</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 12:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47157285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47157285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47157285'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Upstream Linux kernel now merges support for RK3588 and RK3576 video decoders, simplifying driver maintenance and enabling hardware-accelerated video on affected devices. The merge reduces downstream patching and simplifies upstream/downstream collaboration for RK-based platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>上游 Linux 内核现已合并对 RK3588 与 RK3576 视频解码器的支持，简化驱动维护并提升受影响设备的硬件解码能力。此次合并有助于减少下游补丁和简化基于 RK 的平台在上下游协作中的工作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.collabora.com/assets/images/blog/Collabora-RK3588-RK3576.png' data-full='https://www.collabora.com/assets/images/blog/Collabora-RK3588-RK3576.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47154803' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(386) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tachyon.so/blog/sandboxes-wont-save-you' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sandboxes won&#x27;t save you from OpenClaw</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>沙箱不能保护你免受 OpenClaw 攻击</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 09:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47154803' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47154803' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47154803'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that sandboxing alone is insufficient to defend against OpenClaw-style compromises, which can exfiltrate data or break out of containers. It advocates defense-in-depth with hardware isolation, runtime monitoring, and stronger supply-chain controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为单纯的沙箱不足以防御 OpenClaw 之类的漏洞利用，攻击者可能突破容器边界并窃取数据。建议采用多层防御，包括硬件隔离、运行时监控和更强的供应链控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tachyon.so/_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Flogo.png&amp;w=64&amp;q=75' data-full='https://tachyon.so/_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Flogo.png&amp;w=64&amp;q=75' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A nostalgic piece examining how 1993-era computing constraints shaped software workflows, hardware experimentation, and tech culture, framed with a playful time-travel motif. It also reflects on lessons modern developers can draw from those constraints.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47193832' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(389) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/junevm/splathash' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: SplatHash – A lightweight alternative to BlurHash and ThumbHash</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：SplatHash——比 BlurHash/ThumbHash 更轻量的占位哈希</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 03:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47193832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47193832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47193832'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SplatHash provides a compact, fast way to generate placeholder representations for images with minimal computation. It aims to be simpler and smaller than BlurHash or ThumbHash, making it easier to implement in low-resource apps. This leads to snappier placeholders on mobile and web.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SplatHash 提供一种紧凑、快速的方式来生成图片的占位表示，计算成本极低。它力求比 BlurHash 或 ThumbHash 更简单、占用更小，便于在资源受限的应用中实现。这样可以使移动端和网页上的占位图更流畅。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/da195cab103573cf874685c6153d4b47d65b8677272a426733042c8038eef202/junevm/splathash' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/da195cab103573cf874685c6153d4b47d65b8677272a426733042c8038eef202/junevm/splathash' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>类型化汇编语言</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 20, 2026 / 22:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47098029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47098029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47098029'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Typed Assembly Language introduces types into low-level code to enforce safety guarantees at the assembly boundary. This Cornell project explores safeguarding interfaces between high-level type systems and low-level execution, paving paths toward more secure systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>类型化汇编语言在底层代码中引入类型，从而在汇编边界上提供安全保障。该计划将高层类型系统与低层执行结合，帮助推导正确性并提高系统安全性，推动编译器安全设计的研究。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 10:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47155811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47155811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47155811'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains how devirtualization interacts with static polymorphism, showing how compilers can replace dynamic dispatch with static calls for performance gains while preserving polymorphic interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解释去虚拟化如何与静态多态交互，展示编译器如何在保持多态接口的前提下，将动态调度转为静态调用以提升性能。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(401) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://metafixthis.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta problem with URPF our bundle in Boca raton</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Boca Raton 的 URPF 相关元问题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 16:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47130997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47130997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47130997'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This post discusses a meta problem related to URPF (Unicast Reverse Path Forwarding) in a network bundle located in Boca Raton, illustrating how routing policy interactions can complicate security and traffic management. It underscores the importance of careful network configuration and validation to prevent spoofing or packet loss.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中讨论了位于博卡拉顿的网络包中与 URPF（单播返回路径转发）相关的元问题，展示了路由策略交互如何使安全与流量管理变得复杂。强调在网络配置与验证中避免欺骗与数据包丢失的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes a formal framework for dependency resolution to reason about package manager behavior, enabling safer, more flexible strategies. The work clarifies the trade-offs between determinism, performance, and user intent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出了一个依赖解析的形式化模型，用于研究包管理器的行为，便于实现更安全、可组合的策略。该模型揭示了确定性、性能和用户意图之间的权衡。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47131689'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NIST is seeking public comment on AI agent security, inviting input on threat models, governance, and practical controls. The process signals increasing regulatory attention to AI agent safety and interoperability for vendors and users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NIST 正就 AI 助手安全性征求公众意见，邀请就威胁模型、治理与实际控制措施提供反馈。该进程表明监管对 AI 助手安全与互操作性的关注日益增加，影响厂商和用户的设计与部署。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(418) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/desplega-ai/agent-swarm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Agent Swarm – Multi-agent self-learning teams (OSS)</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 04:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47165046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47165046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47165046'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agent Swarm is an OSS framework for coordinating multiple self-learning agents as a team. It illustrates emergent teamwork among AI agents and offers a practical path to building distributed, capable systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个开源框架，用于协同多代理组成团队共同学习。展示了代理间的协作涌现，并提供构建分布式、强大系统的现实路径。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(419) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bZJ7A1QoUEI' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is destroying open source, and it&#x27;s not even good yet [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 正在摧毁开源，甚至还没成熟 [视频]</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 09:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47125019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47125019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47125019'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video arguing that AI-powered coding tools threaten open-source ecosystems, highlighting licensing ambiguity and potential shifts in contribution dynamics. It questions sustainability as AI tools become more capable and pervasive.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>视频讨论了 AI 代码工具可能对开源生态造成的冲击，聚焦许可不清晰与贡献生态的潜在转变。随着 AI 工具能力提升、应用日益广泛，持续性成为关注点。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47202864' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47202864' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(420) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3624725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Science of Detecting LLM-Generated Text (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>识别 LLM 生成文本的科学研究</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 18:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47202864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47202864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47202864'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers survey the methods for detecting text produced by large language models, highlighting the limitations of current classifiers and watermarking approaches. The study shows detectors struggle with newer model generations and domain shift, underscoring that no single method is reliable across all contexts. The takeaway is that robust detection will require a multi-pronged strategy combining statistical cues, watermarking, and human-in-the-loop validation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究评估了现有检测大语言模型生成文本的方法，指出当前分类器和水印等手段的局限性。对最新模型和跨领域文本的检测仍存在显著挑战，单一方法难以全面可靠。关键在于结合统计线索、水印技术以及人工复核的多方位策略来提升鲁棒性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47177700' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47177700' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(421) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://evanhahn.com/introducing-gzpeek/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gzpeek: Tool to Parse Gzip Metadata</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gzpeek：解析 Gzip 元数据的工具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 23:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47177700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47177700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47177700'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gzpeek exposes metadata stored in gzip files, such as header flags, modification times, and optional fields that aren’t always visible in standard tooling. This makes it valuable for forensics, data verification, and debugging pipelines. The tool helps engineers understand data provenance and integrity more precisely.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gzpeek 能揭示 gzip 文件中的元数据，如头部标志、修改时间以及常规工具较少显示的可选字段，适用于取证、数据校验和调试流程。该工具帮助工程师更精确地理解数据来源与完整性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://evanhahn.com/images/logo_white.png' data-full='https://evanhahn.com/images/logo_white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47151367' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47151367' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(422) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pyrefly.org/blog/container-inference-comparison/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Python Type Checker Comparison: Empty Container Inference</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Python 类型检查器对空容器推断的对比</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 05:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47151367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47151367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47151367'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Different Python type checkers vary in how they infer types for empty containers like [] and {}, affecting type-safety and potential false positives. The comparison highlights trade-offs between inference precision and annotation burden, offering practical guidance on choosing a checker and annotating code to improve results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>不同的 Python 类型检查器在对空容器（如 []、{}）的类型推断上差异明显，可能影响类型安全并带来误报。对比揭示了推断精度与标注成本之间的权衡，并就选择检查器与编写提示给出实用建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pyrefly.org/assets/images/empty-container-blog-cef98862cb6b53726eb97df7ee6f2d14.png' data-full='https://pyrefly.org/assets/images/empty-container-blog-cef98862cb6b53726eb97df7ee6f2d14.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(423) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://distrowatch.com/dwres.php?resource=showheadline&amp;story=20140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Manjaro website off-line again due to lapsed certificate</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Manjaro 网站离线再次因证书过期</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 11:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47141385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47141385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47141385'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Manjaro&#x27;s website went offline again after an TLS certificate expired, disrupting access for users and developers. The incident underscores the importance of certificate lifecycle management and automated monitoring for open-source projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Manjaro 官方网站因为证书过期再次离线，影响了用户和开发者的访问。此事件凸显了证书生命周期管理和对开源项目的自动化监控的重要性。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47121352' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47121352' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(424) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/ICCV2025/html/Scheuble_Lidar_Waveforms_are_Worth_40x128x33_Words_ICCV_2025_paper.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lidar waveforms are worth 40x128x33 words</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>激光雷达波形可压缩成 40×128×33 的表示</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 04:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47121352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47121352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47121352'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers show LiDAR waveforms can be represented compactly as 40×128×33 words, enabling more expressive perception with far less data. The approach could accelerate autonomous sensing stacks by reducing data bandwidth and memory requirements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文提出将激光雷达波形紧凑编码为相当于40×128×33个词的表述，便于在更少数据量下实现更丰富的感知能力。此方法有望加速自动驾驶等对雷达数据依赖的感知流水线并降低带宽需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://openaccess.thecvf.com/img/iccv2025-logo.svg' data-full='https://openaccess.thecvf.com/img/iccv2025-logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47106275' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47106275' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(425) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.historytoday.com/archive/making-history/those-who-can-teach-history' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Those who can, teach history</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>那些能讲好历史的人为何应投身教学</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 21, 2026 / 15:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47106275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47106275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47106275'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that people with historical understanding are best placed to teach it, exploring teaching methods, history education access, and public understanding.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47079208' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47079208' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(435) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/baby-chicks-pass-the-bouba-kiki-test-challenging-a-theory-of-language/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Baby chicks pass the bouba-kiki test, challenging a theory of language evolution</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>小鸡通过 Bouba-Kiki 测试，挑战语言进化理论</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 19, 2026 / 12:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47079208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47079208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47079208'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Baby chicks pass the Bouba-Kiki test, suggesting that perceptual biases used to map sounds to shapes may not be unique to humans. The finding questions a central assumption about how language evolves, indicating that some foundational cognitive biases could be shared with other species.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现小鸡也能完成 Bouba‑Kiki 测试，表明将声音与形状关联的感知偏好并非人类独有。此结果对语言进化核心假设提出挑战，提示某些基础认知偏好可能在其他物种中也存在。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/m/3c43d62ae579d094/original/bouba-chicken.jpeg?m=1771525915.006&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/m/3c43d62ae579d094/original/bouba-chicken.jpeg?m=1771525915.006&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47157265' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47157265' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(436) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discourse.threejs.org/t/spacetimedb-threejs-support-and-free-tier/90052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SpacetimeDB ThreeJS Support</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SpacetimeDB 增加 ThreeJS 支持与免费等级</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 12:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47157265' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47157265' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47157265'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SpacetimeDB now supports Three.js, enabling developers to render time-aware data in interactive 3D scenes, plus a free tier lowers the barrier to experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SpacetimeDB 新增对 Three.js 的支持，方便开发者在交互式 3D 场景中可视化时空数据；同时提供免费层，降低上手门槛。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47092809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47092809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47092809'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Georgia&#x27;s wine heritage spans around 8,000 years with ancient winemaking practices, including amphora traditions and early grape varieties.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>格鲁吉亚的葡萄酒传统可追溯至约8000年前，早期就有陶罐发酵等酿酒技艺。文章梳理了当地主要葡萄品种、风格，以及琥珀酒等特色工艺，彰显该国对全球葡萄酒史的影响。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rev-dep, a Go-based tool, claims to rebuild dependency graphs about 20x faster than knip.dev-style alternatives. It demonstrates how language-appropriate tooling can drastically cut Go project build times and improve iteration speed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rev-dep 是一个用 Go 编写的构建工具，宣称在重建依赖图方面比 knip.dev 等替代方案快约 20 倍，显著缩短 Go 项目的开发反馈周期。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(440) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://frankensqlite.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Frankensqlite a Rust reimplementation of SQLite with concurrent writers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Frankensqlite：用 Rust 重实现 SQLite，支持并发写入</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 19:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47176209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47176209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47176209'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Frankensqlite is a Rust reimplementation of SQLite that supports concurrent writers, offering a different concurrency model from the original C-based engine. It showcases Rust&#x27;s safety guarantees in embedded databases and raises questions about compatibility, tooling, and performance trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Frankensqlite 是一个用 Rust 重实现的 SQLite，支持并发写入，展现了 Rust 在嵌入式数据库中的安全性优势。它带来与原生 SQLite 的兼容性、工具链和性能取舍相关的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>汉堡王将用人工智能检查员工是否说‘请’和‘谢谢’</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 05:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47165606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47165606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Burger King plans to deploy AI that analyzes cashier and crew interactions to enforce polite language like &#x27;please&#x27; and &#x27;thank you&#x27;. Critics argue it could enable surveillance and micromanagement, while proponents say it could improve customer service.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>汉堡王计划部署AI，分析收银和员工互动是否使用“请/谢谢”等礼貌用语。批评者担忧监控与微管理，支持者则认为可提升服务质量。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(443) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.danlew.net/2026/01/17/dont-cite-unsold-ebay-listing-prices/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t Cite Unsold eBay Listing Prices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不要引用未售出的 eBay 列价</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 08:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47182247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47182247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47182247'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Don&#x27;t Cite Unsold eBay Listing Prices argues against using unsold listings as price signals, since those prices rarely reflect what items actually fetch. The post advocates more reliable valuation approaches and better data hygiene for price benchmarking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章反对以未售出商品的eBay挂牌价作为价格信号，因为这些价格往往并不反映成交价。作者倡导更可靠的估值方法和更清洁的数据来做价格基准。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(444) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jamesoswald.dev/posts/bigoinequality/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is f(x) ≤ g(x) + O(1)? Inequalities With Asymptotics</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 20, 2026 / 05:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47088076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47088076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post explains how to interpret f(x) ≤ g(x) + O(1) in asymptotic analysis, clarifying how constant terms affect practical bounds. It helps distinguish between tight mathematical bounds and real-world performance implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章讲解了渐近分析中 f(x) ≤ g(x) + O(1) 的含义，阐明常数项在实际界限中的影响，帮助区分严格的数学界限与实际性能的关系。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(448) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/02/23/science/evolution-vertebrate-eye.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The rise of eyes began with just one</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47125484'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A feature on how eyes evolved as complex organs, tracing how early photoreceptor cells diversified into diverse eye types across species. It highlights the modular nature of biological innovation and how a single starting point can lead to vast diversity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章讲述眼睛作为复杂器官的进化过程，追溯早期光感受器细胞如何在不同物种中演化出多样的眼部结构，强调生物创新的模块化特性以及一个起点如何孕育出广泛的多样性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(451) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/FirebirdSQL/decimal-java' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decimal-Java is a library to convert java.math.BigDecimal to and from IEEE-754r</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Decimal-Java：在 Java BigDecimal 与 IEEE-754r 之间转换的库</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 01:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47134779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47134779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47134779'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Decimal-Java provides conversion between BigDecimal and IEEE-754r decimal floating-point representation, addressing precision concerns for financial and scientific apps. It simplifies interoperability between Java and decimal standards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Decimal-Java 提供 BigDecimal 与 IEEE-754r 十进制浮点表示之间的双向转换，旨在解决金融与科学应用中的精度问题，并促进 Java 与十进制标准的互操作性。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47197003' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Open Source,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47197003' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(454) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rivet-dev/rivet' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: SQLite for Rivet Actors – one database per agent, tenant, or document</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rivet Actors 的 SQLite——每个代理/租户/文档一个数据库</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 08:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47197003'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SQLite for Rivet Actors proposes one database per agent, tenant, or document, enabling clean isolation in multi-agent systems. It offers simplicity and fault containment, while trading off cross-entity querying and management headaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为 Rivet Actors 引入每个代理、租户或文档一个数据库的 SQLite 方案，实现多代理系统中的清晰隔离。简化性与故障隔离性有优势，但跨实体查询与管理成本可能上升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/654560183/8fa02fa0-32a0-4700-9d17-26966325db09' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/654560183/8fa02fa0-32a0-4700-9d17-26966325db09' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47140332' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47140332' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(455) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47140332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Tag Promptless on any GitHub PR/Issue to get updated user-facing docs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：在任意 GitHub PR/Issue 上标记 Promptless 以获得更新的用户文档</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 10:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47140332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47140332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47140332'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Show HN project uses a &#x27;Promptless&#x27; label on GitHub PRs and issues to trigger updates to user-facing docs. It showcases how lightweight metadata can help keep documentation aligned with code changes and reduce drift.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这个 Show HN 项目在任意 GitHub 的 PR/Issue 上打上“Promptless”标签，以触发用户文档的生成或同步。它展示了通过简单元数据实现文档与代码变更保持一致的思路，减少文档漂移。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47195530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47195530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47195530'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: Decided to play god this morning, so I built an agent civilisation, detailing architecture, behaviors, and the challenges of simulating autonomous agents at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN：今晨决定扮演上帝，于是搭建了一个代理文明，分享架构、行为设计与大规模仿真自主代理的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47154962' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47154962' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(457) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lackofimagination.org/2026/02/time-travel-debugging-replaying-production-bugs-locally/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Time-Travel Debugging: Replaying Production Bugs Locally</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>时光穿梭调试：在本地重现生产环境中的错误</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 09:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47154962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47154962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47154962'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Time-travel debugging describes deterministic replay of production bugs locally, enabling developers to diagnose issues without deploying to prod. Techniques include capturing inputs, state, and logs to faithfully reproduce issues.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>时光穿梭调试实现了对生产环境错误的可确定性重放，使开发者能够在本地诊断问题而无需部署到生产环境。文中介绍了捕获输入、状态和日志等方法，确保问题能够被准确重现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lackofimagination.org/images/hasnain-sikora-41NsEMAB1Ps-unsplash.jpg' data-full='https://lackofimagination.org/images/hasnain-sikora-41NsEMAB1Ps-unsplash.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI doomsday-type report rattles markets, highlighting perceived risks of runaway automation and systemic threats while urging governance. The story illustrates how sentiment around AI risk can influence markets and policy debates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份 AI 末日报告震撼市场，强调对失控自动化和系统性风险的担忧并呼吁治理框架。报道反映了对 AI 风险的情绪如何影响市场与监管讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>利用电子显微镜技术实现的微型二维码</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 20, 2026 / 20:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47097405' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47097405' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47097405'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrated a QR code at micrometer scale using electron microscopy, pushing nano-scale data encoding into the realm of physical labels. The result hints at future applications in nanotech tagging and anti-counterfeit schemes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者展示了利用电子显微镜技术实现的微型二维码，推动纳米尺度数据编码及在标签、溯源等领域的潜在应用。</p>
