<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A post linking graph topology concepts with battle royale mechanics, showing how pruning and beam search strategies can model territory control and player dynamics. It offers takeaways for game AI and algorithm design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>博文将图的拓扑结构与大逃杀机制联系起来，展示如何用束搜索和图剪枝等技术建模地图控制与玩家动态。为游戏人工智能与算法设计提供可借鉴的思路。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(465) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aishortcutlab.com/articles/pwc-ceo-survey-2026-only-12-of-ceos-win-with-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>56% of CEOs report zero financial return from AI in 2026 (PwC survey, n=4,454)</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47174891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47174891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47174891'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PwC&#x27;s 2026 survey shows 56% of CEOs report zero AI-driven financial return, signaling feedback that AI investments are not yet translating into tangible value for most organizations. The results underscore the need for better alignment to business outcomes and governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PwC 的 2026 年调查显示，56% 的 CEO 表示 AI 未带来金融回报，表明大多数组织尚未将 AI 投资转化为可量化的价值。结果强调需要将 AI 与具体业务成果及治理结构更好地对齐。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47186444' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47186444' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(466) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://emt.pensoft.net/article/185117/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Otters as Bioindicators of Estuarine Health</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>水獭作为河口健康的生物指示物</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 14:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47186444' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47186444' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47186444'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Otters are studied as bioindicators to assess estuarine health, correlating otter presence with water quality, prey availability, and habitat integrity. The research supports using charismatic megafauna as practical proxies for ecosystem status, aiding conservation and policy decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究将水獭作为河口健康的生物指示物，将水獭栖息情况与水质、猎物资源和栖息地完整性相关联。此类研究支持以具吸引力的大型动物作为生态系统状态的代理指标，帮助制定保护和管理政策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://emt.pensoft.net/showimg/d200x_1525594.jpg' data-full='https://emt.pensoft.net/showimg/d200x_1525594.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47133716' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47133716' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(467) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ufo5260987423/scheme-langserver' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Scheme-langserver – Digest incomplete code with static analysis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Scheme-langserver——通过静态分析提取不完整代码的语言服务器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 22:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47133716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47133716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47133716'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scheme-langserver provides static-analysis-based digesting of incomplete Scheme code to aid editor tooling. It demonstrates how lightweight static analysis can support development in niche languages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Scheme-langserver 利用静态分析对不完整的 Scheme 代码进行摘要，帮助编辑器工具链。该项目展示了轻量级静态分析在小众语言开发中的可行性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/70dfa0b5f4c82c6e1e4c54c3d89f5f28ebe4902fa5408e87532999a33a8554ab/ufo5260987423/scheme-langserver' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/70dfa0b5f4c82c6e1e4c54c3d89f5f28ebe4902fa5408e87532999a33a8554ab/ufo5260987423/scheme-langserver' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(468) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47105086' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Programmable Watches with WiFi?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>带 WiFi 的可编程智能手表？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 21, 2026 / 13:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47105086' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47105086' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47105086'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>People discuss feasibility, power budgets, and security implications of Wi‑Fi capable programmable smartwatches. The conversation touches on microcontroller limits, firmware ecosystems, and potential energy trade-offs for always-on connectivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论带 Wi‑Fi 的可编程智能手表的可行性、能耗和安全性。话题涉及微控单元、固件生态，以及始终保持联网对小型佩戴设备的能源权衡与开发生态。</p>

</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47171773'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cronboard provides a terminal-based dashboard to manage cron jobs, offering a lightweight CLI alternative to web dashboards for visibility and control over scheduled tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cronboard 提供一个终端仪表板，用于管理 cron 作业，作为对网页仪表板的轻量化 CLI 替代，提升对计划任务的可视化和控制。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 05:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47165602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47165602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47165602'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mission Control is an open-source task manager tailored for AI agents, enabling teams to orchestrate and track agent workflows. It aims to bring more structured governance and collaboration to autonomous AI systems, open for community contributions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mission Control 是一个面向 AI 代理的开源任务管理工具，帮助团队编排并跟踪代理工作流。它旨在为自治 AI 系统带来更结构化的治理与协作，欢迎社区贡献。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨卡拉瓦乔与拉图尔在巴洛克绘画中如何通过光线引导观众注意力与叙事。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(472) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sites.stat.columbia.edu/jcliu/paper/GSPRT_SQA3.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Generalized Sequential Probability Ratio Test for Families of Hypotheses [pdf]</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47085061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47085061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47085061'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A statistical note introduces GSPRT for testing multiple hypotheses in a sequential framework, useful for streaming data, adaptive experimentation, or real-time decision-making.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文介绍了广义序贯似然比检验（GSPRT）在一族假设的序贯框架下的应用，适用于流数据、自适应实验和实时决策。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47169717' data-tags='Gaming,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47169717' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(473) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/02/26/style/stardew-valley-video-game-anniversary' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stardew Valley at 10: The pixel art farm game that became an unlikely phenomenon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>星露谷物语十周年：这款像素风农场游戏的非同寻常现象</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 10:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47169717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47169717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47169717'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ten years after its release, Stardew Valley remains a cult indie hit thanks to tight systems, moddability, and warm pixel art. Its success reshaped indie publishing, inspired a wave of imitators, and influenced how developers approach sustainability and community engagement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>十周年之际，星露谷物语凭借精巧的系统、广泛的模组支持和温暖的像素美学成为现象级独立游戏。它改变了独立发行的格局，影响了后续作品的开发与社区互动的持续性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/img-3011.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' data-full='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/img-3011.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47169146' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47169146' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(474) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deepmind.google/models/synthid/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SynthID</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SynthID：合成媒体身份识别技术</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 09:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47169146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47169146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47169146'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SynthID introduces a robust watermarking approach for AI-generated content to help verify authenticity and provenance. It’s a step toward combating misinformation, but also foreshadows an arms race between watermarking and evasion and raises questions about policy, ethics, and user trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SynthID 提出了一种稳健的水印机制，用以标识 AI 生成内容的真实性与来源。这是打击错误信息的一步，但也预示着水印对抗技术的竞赛，以及对政策、道德与用户信任的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/AXiIx9H8Zbs0ao0jpyayhmTZFIB-rrnWT-fFReylThnuio6QhOpEF8s2nj7OSai-L1bdoKeDu1ZNITnFLqvsKrnb5vPqlssdyKE83atw7A=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/AXiIx9H8Zbs0ao0jpyayhmTZFIB-rrnWT-fFReylThnuio6QhOpEF8s2nj7OSai-L1bdoKeDu1ZNITnFLqvsKrnb5vPqlssdyKE83atw7A=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47093959' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(476) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://harpers.org/archive/2026/03/lords-of-the-ring-joshua-hunt-cultural-politics-sumo-wrestling/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lords of the Ring</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>擂台之王：文化政治、相扑与权力</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 20, 2026 / 13:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47093959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47093959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47093959'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An examination of power dynamics in sumo culture and its intersection with politics and media. The piece uses sumo as a lens to discuss how tradition and modernity collide in national identity and cultural influence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过对相扑文化中的权力结构及其与政治、媒体的交叉影响进行考察，以相扑作为镜头探讨传统与现代性在国家认同与文化影响力中的冲突与融合。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wp.harpers.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/CUT-22-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://wp.harpers.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/CUT-22-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47144835' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47144835' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(477) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jstor.org/stable/1721427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Long Is the Coast of Britain? (1967)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国海岸线有多长？（1967）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 15:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47144835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47144835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47144835'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>How Long Is the Coast of Britain? (1967) is a landmark discussion in fractal geometry showing that coastline length depends on measurement scale, introducing the concept of fractal dimension and scale invariance that reshaped how we model natural forms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1967 年的这篇论文成为分形几何的里程碑，揭示海岸线长度取决于测量尺度，并引入分形维数与尺度不变性的概念，改变了我们建模自然现象的方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47103668' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47103668' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(478) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/full/10.1080/17452759.2026.2613185' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>3D-Printed electric motor via multi-modal, multi-material extrusion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过多模态多材料挤出技术实现的 3D 打印电动机</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(480) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mksglu/claude-context-mode' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Context Mode – 315 KB of MCP output becomes 5.4 KB in Claude Code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Context Mode—315 KB 的 MCP 输出在 Claude Code 中压缩为 5.4 KB</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 24, 2026 / 22:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47148025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47148025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47148025'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Context Mode demonstrates dramatic output compression in Claude Code, shrinking 315 KB of MCP output down to 5.4 KB while preserving essential context. This signals a breakthrough for memory-heavy coding tasks, enabling leaner contexts and potential speedups in code reasoning and generation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Context Mode 展示了在 Claude Code 中对 MCP 输出的显著压缩，将 315 KB 的输出压缩至 5.4 KB，同时保留关键上下文。这一做法对需要大量上下文的代码任务具有重要意义，能降低内存占用并提升推理效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6d610b627a46d785be7d7a5a6ec2a8df9f7bb4b929de6e0982f189e777eabddf/mksglu/claude-context-mode' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6d610b627a46d785be7d7a5a6ec2a8df9f7bb4b929de6e0982f189e777eabddf/mksglu/claude-context-mode' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(486) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://theoryofconstraints.blogspot.com/2007/06/toc-stories-2-blue-light-creating.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Theory of Constraints: &quot;Blue Light&quot; creating capacity for nothing (2007)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>约束理论：&#x27;蓝光&#x27; 让产能看起来存在却无实际产出（2007）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 09:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47183191' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47183191' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47183191'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Theory of Constraints: &#x27;Blue Light&#x27; creates apparent capacity without real throughput, a cautionary note about buffered capacity inflating productivity metrics in operations research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>约束理论中的“蓝光”现象指通过缓冲容量制造表面产能，但并未带来实际产出，提醒运营研究中缓冲容量可能夸大生产力指标。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(489) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/music/2026/feb/26/steven-isserlis-on-the-formidable-gyorgy-kurtag-at-100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Play like a dog biting God&#x27;s feet&#x27;: Steven Isserlis on György Kurtág at 100</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“像狗咬上帝的脚一样演奏”：史蒂文·艾瑟里斯谈 György Kurtág 百周年</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 26, 2026 / 11:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47170847' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47170847' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47170847'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Steven Isserlis offers a vivid perspective on Kurtág&#x27;s music in the composer’s centennial year. The piece delves into Kurtág&#x27;s economy of expression and how performers navigate his compact, intensely expressive lines, offering insights into contemporary classical technique.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大提琴家史蒂文·艾瑟里斯在库塔格百周年之际，提出对库塔格音乐的生动解读。文章深入探讨库塔格简练而强烈的表达方式，以及演奏者如何驾驭其紧凑、极具表现力的乐句，为当代古典演奏技巧提供洞见。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(496) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ellanew.com/2026/03/02/ptpl-197-record-retrieve-from-a-personal-knowledgebase' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to record and retrieve anything you&#x27;ve ever had to look up twice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>记录并检索你曾多次查找过的任何信息</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 20:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47213819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47213819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47213819'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal knowledge-base approach that captures repeated lookups into a searchable archive, making it easy to retrieve past queries without re-remembering details. It emphasizes ownership and organization of your own search history, enabling faster discovery and reducing cognitive load. This could inform tools and workflows for knowledge workers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过把重复查找的信息纳入可搜索的个人知识库，帮助用户轻松检索历史查询，避免重复记忆。该方法强调对自身搜索历史的拥有和组织，提升检索效率、降低认知负担。它还可能为知识工作者的工作流提供新思路。</p>

They&#x27;re Enforcing It</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科技公司不仅鼓励员工使用 AI，还在强制执行</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47147513' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47147513' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article reports on tech firms mandating AI usage for employees, with policies, governance, and enforcement measures shaping daily workflows and productivity. It discusses potential benefits and risks, including privacy, compliance, and the shifting power dynamics as AI becomes a standard tool in the workplace.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章报道科技公司正在强制要求员工使用 AI，并设立治理与执行机制来规范日常工作。这带来潜在的生产力提升，同时也引发隐私、合规和职场权力结构的变化等风险与讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(505) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://contalign.jefflunt.com/self-improving-software/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Self-improving software won&#x27;t produce Skynet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自我改进的软件不会催生天网</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 19:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47161498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47161498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47161498'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Recursive self-improvement isn&#x27;t a free path to Skynet. Real-world systems face safety, alignment, and governance constraints that limit runaway upgrading. Without careful design, automated improvements can be brittle or misaligned.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>递归式自我改进并非通往天网的捷径。现实系统受安全、对齐与治理等约束，限制了升级的失控速度。若缺乏周密设计，自动化的改进可能变得脆弱或与目标偏离。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(509) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tomoshibi.in-hakumei.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Tomoshibi – A writing app where your words fade by firelight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Tomoshibi——在火光中逐渐淡出的写作应用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 09:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47197735'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tomoshibi is a writing app where words fade by firelight, creating a contemplative writing experience. The project explores how temporality and focus constraints shape creative workflow in the terminal.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tomoshibi 是一款让文字在火光中逐渐淡出的写作应用，营造沉思式写作体验。它探索时间性与专注约束如何影响终端环境下的创作流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tomoshibi.in-hakumei.com/lp/og-image.png' data-full='https://tomoshibi.in-hakumei.com/lp/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47160980' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(510) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Intrect-io/OpenSwarm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: OpenSwarm – Multi‑Agent Claude CLI Orchestrator for Linear/GitHub</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 18:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47160980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47160980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47160980'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenSwarm is a multi-agent CLI orchestrator for Claude, enabling coordinated tasks across agents and tools. It demonstrates practical workflow composition for LLM-powered automation with GitHub integration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenSwarm 提供一个用于 Claude 的多代理 CLI 调度器，能让多个代理协同完成任务并接入工具链。展示了将 LLM 驱动的工作流与 GitHub 等工具整合的实际方法。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47127838' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47127838' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(511) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/how-can-infinity-come-in-many-sizes-20260223/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Can Infinity Come in Many Sizes?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无穷大可以有多种尺寸吗？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 11:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47127838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47127838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47127838'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the concept that infinity comes in many sizes through set theory, discussing cardinalities and the distinction between countable and uncountable infinities with approachable examples.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从集合论角度解释无穷大并非单一量级，而是有多种不同的大小，通过基数比较与可数/不可数之分，给出易懂的示例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/Infinity-Explainer-cr-Mark-BelanMichael-Kanyongolo-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/Infinity-Explainer-cr-Mark-BelanMichael-Kanyongolo-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(512) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npr.org/2026/02/27/nx-s1-5720167/trump-ice-immigration-social-media-deportation-dhs-immigrants-detained-disputes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How the Federal Government Is Painting Immigrants as Criminals on Social Media</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>联邦政府如何在社交媒体上将移民描绘成罪犯</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 05:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47206573'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Investigations show government agencies using social media to portray immigrants as criminals, raising concerns about misinformation, civil rights, and political signaling. The coverage discusses impact on communities, enforcement practices, and trust in online discourse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查显示政府机构在社交媒体上将移民描绘成罪犯，引发关于错误信息、公民权利与政治信号传递的担忧。报道分析对社区的影响、执法做法及公众信任的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(514) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asteriskmag.com/issues/13/seeing-like-a-sedan' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Seeing Like a Sedan</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>像轿车一样看世界</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47153797' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47153797' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47153797'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Seeing Like a Sedan argues that familiar design and framing influence how we build and experience technology, using car-centric thinking to critique urban and software infrastructures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以轿车视角审视设计思维如何影响技术构建与使用，借此批判城市与软件基础设施背后的隐性假设。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47155548' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47155548' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(515) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fireborn.mataroa.blog/blog/the-slow-death-of-the-power-user/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Slow Death of the Power User</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>强力用户的慢慢消退</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 10:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47155548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47155548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47155548'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay argues that the &#x27;power user&#x27; archetype is fading as products hide complexity behind opinionated defaults, cloud services, and safety rails. It cautions that technology&#x27;s productivity gains come at the cost of deeper control and customization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为“强力用户”的形象正在消退，产品通过默认设定、云服务和安全策略隐藏了复杂性。作者警示技术在带来生产力的同时，牺牲了深度控制和定制能力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47113946' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47113946' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(516) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eventual-consistency.vercel.app/posts/write-locks-faster' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Read Locks Are Not Your Friends</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>读锁不是你的朋友</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 22, 2026 / 11:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47113946'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that read locks can hinder concurrency and lead to performance issues in distributed systems; it suggests alternative synchronization patterns like lock-free data structures or optimistic concurrency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为读锁可能阻碍并发、导致分布式系统的性能问题；建议采用无锁数据结构或乐观并发等替代同步模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://eventual-consistency.vercel.app/_astro/locking-comparison.DzgmJbDS_Z2o2OQV.webp' data-full='https://eventual-consistency.vercel.app/_astro/locking-comparison.DzgmJbDS_Z2o2OQV.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47109058' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47109058' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(517) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quasi-Zenith_Satellite_System' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Quasi-Zenith Satellite System</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>准天顶卫星系统（QZSS）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 21, 2026 / 23:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47109058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47109058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47109058'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Quasi-Zenith Satellite System augments GPS to improve positioning in challenging environments and urban canyons. It represents a regional effort to boost location accuracy for users in Japan and nearby areas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>准天顶卫星系统通过区域卫星导航增强 GPS，提升在高楼林立区域的定位精度。该系统主要覆盖日本及周边区域，提升本地化能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/9/94/QZSS_logo.png' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/9/94/QZSS_logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47108894' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(518) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mahaloz.re/dec-history-pt1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>30 Years of Decompilation and the Unsolved Structuring Problem: Part 1 (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三十年的反编译与未解决的结构化问题：第一部分（2024）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 21, 2026 / 22:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47108894' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47108894' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47108894'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on decompilation history, focusing on the ongoing challenge of recovering high-level structure from binaries. It discusses why structural organization remains hard and how tooling is evolving to bridge the gap.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾性论文聚焦反编译史中的长期难题：如何从二进制中还原高级结构。讨论为何结构化组织仍然困难，以及工具如何演进以缩小这一差距。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(519) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://httptoolkit.com/blog/dictionary-compression-performance-zstd-brotli/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dictionary Compression is finally here, and it&#x27;s ridiculously good</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>字典压缩技术终于问世，性能提升惊人</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 23, 2026 / 04:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47121233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47121233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47121233'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A dictionary-based compression approach arrives with surprisingly strong performance gains over traditional methods, potentially reducing bandwidth and storage costs across systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于字典的压缩方法带来出色的性能提升，可能显著降低系统的带宽与存储成本，对各类应用场景都具潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47211914' data-tags='Show HN,Security,Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47211914' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(520) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/melonattacker/logira' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Logira – eBPF runtime auditing for AI agent runs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Logira——用于 AI 代理运行的 eBPF 运行时审计</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 15:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47211914' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47211914' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47211914'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Logira introduces eBPF-based runtime auditing for AI agent runs, offering visibility into actions and a layer of security for AI workflows.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(525) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260226-how-ai-can-read-your-thoughts' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Evolving descriptive text of mental content from human brain activity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从脑部活动中生成描述性文本</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 21:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47214250' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 14</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47214250' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47214250'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers continue to evolve methods that translate brain signals into descriptive text, inching toward more natural and expressive brain-computer interfaces. The advances enable richer glimpses into mental content while raising questions about privacy, consent, and how we measure &#x27;thought&#x27; in real time. These developments broaden potential uses from communication aids to neurotech monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者正在改进将脑信号转化为描述性文本的方法，向更自然、富表达的脑机接口迈进。相关进展使对思维内容的理解更丰富，同时引发隐私、知情同意以及如何实时衡量“思想”的讨论。这些发展拓展了从信息传递帮助到神经技术监测的潜在应用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(530) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.vox.com/climate/480083/beef-agriculture-deforestation-amazon-rainforest' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Foods destroying rainforests, in one simple chart</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 07:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47207580' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47207580' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A simple chart links a small set of foods—beef, soy, palm oil, and others—to rainforest deforestation, outlining the supply-chain drivers and environmental impact. The piece emphasizes how consumer choices and production systems drive ecological harm and calls for sustainable sourcing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一张简单图表将少数食品（如牛肉、大豆、棕榈油等）与雨林砍伐联系起来，梳理供给链驱动因素及环境影响。文章强调消费选择与生产体系对生态破坏的推动作用，并呼吁更可持续的采购。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(531) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.axios.com/local/austin/2026/03/02/waymo-vehicle-blocks-ems-austin-mass-shooting' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Waymo robotaxi blocks EMS responding to Austin mass shooting</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FCaptcha proposes a modern CAPTCHA system with enhanced bot-detection capabilities and adaptive challenges designed to outpace automated solvers.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47210357' data-tags='Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47210357' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(535) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cyloq.se/en/research/cve-2026-0714-tpm-sniffing-luks-keys-on-an-embedded-device' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TPM-Sniffing LUKS Keys on an Embedded Linux Device [CVE-2026-0714]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在嵌入式 Linux 设备上嗅探 LUKS 密钥的 TPM 漏洞（CVE-2026-0714）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 01, 2026 / 12:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47210357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 9</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47210357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47210357'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A vulnerability allows TPM to leak LUKS passphrases on certain embedded devices, enabling offline decryption under conditions. Exploits require trusted hardware, but firmware updates and mitigations are essential for affected devices. The bug highlights the fragility of hardware-backed encryption when firmware is compromised.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个漏洞允许 TPM 在某些嵌入式设备上泄露 LUKS 密码短语，条件成立时可进行离线解密。攻击需要可信硬件，但固件更新和缓解措施对受影响设备至关重要。这一漏洞凸显了在固件被攻破时，硬件背后的加密也可能脆弱。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/67cabfc40d4754219fea5744/681a094cbc4dee0892d46119_Swedish.avif' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/67cabfc40d4754219fea5744/681a094cbc4dee0892d46119_Swedish.avif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47181714' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(536) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spiraldb.com/post/cascading-compression-with-btrblocks' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Have your cake and decompress it too</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>同时实现高压缩与快速解压的级联压缩方法</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 27, 2026 / 07:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 9</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47181714'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cascading compression technique demonstrates how to chain multiple compression layers to achieve small on-disk size while preserving fast random access. The approach is relevant for large datasets, backups, and systems where storage and latency both matter.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>级联压缩方法展示了将多种压缩层级串联起来，在保持快速随机访问的同时实现更小的磁盘占用。该思路对大数据集、备份和需要兼顾存储与延迟的系统具有实际意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spiraldb.com/images/posts/cascading-compression-with-btrblocks.jpg' data-full='https://spiraldb.com/images/posts/cascading-compression-with-btrblocks.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47198099'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CSP for Pentesters covers the fundamentals of Content Security Policy—directives, nonce management, reporting, and testing strategies. It shows how to assess and harden policies against common web vulnerabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本指南讲解内容安全策略（CSP）的基础要点，包括指令、Nonce 管理、报告与测试策略，帮助评估并强化对常见网页漏洞的防护。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47149348' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(539) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cosmo.tardis.ac/files/2026-01-27-activation-2.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Better Activation Functions for NNUE</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NNUE 的激活函数改进</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 25, 2026 / 01:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47149348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 5</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47149348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47149348'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New activation functions for NNUE yield measurable gains in accuracy and efficiency for chess engines, especially on CPU. This helps improve evaluation speed and reduces energy use on mid-range hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NNUE 的激活函数改进在棋类引擎中带来准确性和效率的提升，特别是在 CPU 上的评估速度得到改善，同时降低中端硬件的能耗。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47203544' data-tags='Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47203544' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(540) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newscientist.com/article/2517389-human-brain-cells-on-a-chip-learned-to-play-doom-in-a-week/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Human brain cells on a chip learned to play Doom in a week</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>芯片上的人脑细胞一周内学会玩 Doom</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Feb 28, 2026 / 19:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47203544' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 4</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47203544' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47203544'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In a chip-based brain-cell experiment, cultures learned to play Doom within a week, illustrating rapid learning-like behavior in biological substrates and implications for neuromorphic research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在芯片上的脑细胞培养物一周内学会玩 Doom，展示了生物材料中近似快速学习的能力，对神经形态研究具有潜在意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.newscientist.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/27124116/SEI_287024727.jpg' data-full='https://images.newscientist.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/27124116/SEI_287024727.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
